TL;DR: 本文探索了Transformer语法中句法树的多种线性化遍历策略（DFT、BFT及新型混合策略PRT），揭示了嵌套组合与全局前瞻之间的内在权衡，并提供了任务感知的TG设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer语法研究仅依赖深度优先遍历（DFT）对句法树进行线性化，但遍历方式对模型性能有显著影响，因此需要探索更广泛的遍历设计空间。

Method: 提出了广度优先遍历（BFT）和一种新型混合遍历策略——产生式规则遍历（PRT），该策略结合了BFT的结构前瞻与DFT的早期词汇生成。将不同的遍历方法与变化的树配置和掩码策略集成，进行语言建模、句法泛化和摘要任务的实证评估。

Result: 揭示了嵌套组合与全局前瞻之间的内在权衡，不同遍历策略在不同任务上表现不同，不存在放之四海而皆准的最优方案。

Conclusion: 提供了设计任务感知型Transformer语法的可操作建议，表明应根据具体任务需求选择适合的线性化遍历策略。

Abstract: Transformer Grammars (TGs) enhance language modeling by incorporating syntactic tree structures. Despite the potentially significant impact on model performance of how syntactic trees are linearized in TGs, existing studies rely solely on Depth-First Traversal (DFT) for linearization. In this paper, we expand the traversal design space by exploring Breadth-First Traversal (BFT) and a novel hybrid traversal strategy, Production-Rule Traversal (PRT), which combines the structural lookahead of BFT with the early lexical generation of DFT. We integrate these traversal methods with varying tree configurations and masking strategies, and empirically evaluate their performance on language modeling, syntactic generalization and summarization. We reveal the inherent trade-offs between nested composition and global lookahead, providing actionable recommendations for designing task-aware Transformer Grammars.

</details>


### [124] [Data-Driven Decoding of Russell's Circumplex Model of Affect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16843)
*Amdjed Belaref,Samir Sadok,Zineb Noumir,Renaud Seguier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究Transformer模型（RoBERTa、wav2vec 2.0及多模态融合架构）的嵌入表示是否内在地编码了Russell情绪环状模型中的几何规律性。多模态融合实现了与Russell情绪排序的完美拓扑对齐，零样本设置下投影的细粒度情绪词也接近人类映射坐标，证明情绪环状结构是模型嵌入的内在属性而非仅源于人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感计算中深度学习模型常被视为不透明的黑盒，其潜在空间是否具备心理学理论中的情绪几何结构尚不清楚。本文旨在验证Transformer嵌入是否能够复现Russell环状模型的价态-唤醒度拓扑规律。

Method: 通过两个互补实验，在文本（RoBERTa）、语音（wav2vec 2.0）及多模态融合Transformer架构上提取深度表示，使用MSP-Podcast自然数据集和LLM生成的控制刺激，评估潜在空间与Russell环状模型拓扑结构的一致性以及类人邻域关系的复现程度。

Result: 多模态文本-语音融合实现了与Russell情绪排序的完美拓扑对齐；零样本设置下利用通用文本嵌入投影的细粒度情绪词接近人类标注坐标。

Conclusion: Russell环状结构是模型嵌入内在编码的属性，而非人工标注的副产品。本文提出了一种数据驱动的情绪模型验证框架，弥合了心理学理论与表示学习之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Affective computing increasingly relies on deep learning to represent emotions, yet latent spaces often remain opaque, high-dimensional black boxes. This paper investigates whether Transformers' embeddings recover the geometric regularities of Russell's circumplex model. We unify two complementary experiments testing the hypothesis that, after training models on text and speech, their resulting latent spaces encode a topology consistent with valence-arousal and reproduce human-like neighborhood relations. Specifically, we evaluate deep representations extracted from Transformer-based text (RoBERTa) and speech (wav2vec 2.0) encoders, along with a multimodal Transformer fusion architecture, across naturalistic datasets like MSP-Podcast and controlled LLM-generated stimuli. Our analysis reveals that multimodal fusion of text and audio yields perfect topological alignment with Russell's primary emotion ordering. Furthermore, in a zero-shot setting using generic text embeddings, projected fine-grained emotion terms fall close to their established human-mapped coordinates. Our contribution is a novel, data-driven framework for validating emotion models, demonstrating that Russell's circumplex structure is intrinsically encoded in the embeddings of these modalities rather than being solely an artifact of human labeling, thereby bridging the gap between psychological theory and representation learning.

</details>


### [125] [Robust Dual-Signal Fusion: Hybrid Neuro-Symbolic Gating with Compressed Chain-of-Thought Refinement for Irony Detection in Social Media Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16845)
*Ankit Bhattacharjee,Krityapriya Bhaumik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出鲁棒双信号融合框架，通过混合神经符号架构压缩思维链推理轨迹，在无监督微调下实现与微调模型相当的讽刺检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型默认倾向于字面语义解释，零样本讽刺检测一直是一个持久挑战。

Method: RDS框架是一种混合神经符号架构，通过压缩思维链推理轨迹实现讽刺检测，无需监督微调。

Result: 在TweetEval测试集上准确率78.1%，Macro F1为0.777；在iSarcasm数据集上零样本Macro F1为0.6726，Ironic F1为0.4821；统计消融实验证实三信号并发融合方可显著提升性能(p=0.005)。

Conclusion: RDS框架在零样本讽刺检测中达到与微调模型相当的性能，且结构协同效应是性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) natively default to literal semantic interpretations, making zero-shot irony detection a persistent challenge. We introduce the Robust Dual-Signal (RDS) Fusion framework, a hybrid neuro-symbolic architecture that compresses Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories without Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT). Evaluated on a strictly held-out TweetEval test set (N=734), RDS achieves 78.1% accuracy and a Macro F1 of 0.777, matching the absolute performance ceiling of the fine-tuned BERTweet. On the heavily imbalanced iSarcasm dataset, the frozen CoT pipeline filters 22.5% of out-of-distribution hallucinations, yielding a zero-shot Macro F1 of 0.6726 and Ironic F1 of 0.4821, outperforming multiple heavily supervised SemEval transformer ensembles. A statistical ablation confirms this structural synergy: adding the symbolic prior to the neural baseline yields no significant gain (p = 0.242), and the marginal benefit of adding the CoT pipeline to that prior is heavily compressed (p = 0.149). Only the complete, concurrent fusion of all three signals achieves a statistically validated improvement over the baseline (p = 0.005).

</details>


### [126] [Follow the Latent Roadmap: Navigating Revocable Decoding for Diffusion LLMs with Anchor Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16847)
*Yizhen Yao,Qinglin Zhu,Runcong Zhao,Xiangxiang Dai,Yanzheng Xiang,Yulan He,Lin Gui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASRD，一种无需训练的锚点监督可撤销解码框架，通过解耦扩散语言模型中的可信锚点令牌和不确定候选令牌，利用锚点引导生成和锚点扰动验证两种机制，在数学和编程任务上实现了高达6.4%的精度提升和7.2倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型(dLLMs)在并行生成中面临解码速度与质量之间的权衡，现有可撤销解码策略在混合质量上下文中会导致错误传播和局部错误强化两种关键失败模式。

Method: ASRD框架包含两个互补机制：(1) 锚点引导生成——通过时间一致性识别锚点令牌，将熵加权的锚点信号注入掩码位置以修正注意力；(2) 锚点扰动验证——对不确定候选令牌施加正交扰动，破坏脆弱的局部共识以暴露并重新掩码错误。

Result: 在数学和编程基准测试中，ASRD相比最新的重新掩码基线方法，准确率提升高达6.4%，推理吞吐量提升高达7.2倍。

Conclusion: ASRD作为一种无需训练的框架，通过在嵌入空间中显式解耦解码上下文，有效缓解了错误传播和局部错误强化问题，显著提升了dLLMs的生成质量和推理效率。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) offer a promising avenue for parallel generation but face a trade-off between decoding speed and quality. While revocable decoding strategies attempt to mitigate errors by verifying and remasking tokens, they typically operate within a mixed-quality context. This leads to two critical failures: \textit{Error Propagation}, where new tokens absorb toxic information from erroneous context, and \textit{Local Error Reinforcement}, where errors mutually reinforce each other to evade detection. To alleviate these challenges, we propose ASRD (Anchor Supervised Revocable Decoding), a training-free framework that operates within the embedding space. ASRD explicitly decouples the decoding context into trusted \textit{Anchor Tokens}, which are identified via temporal consistency, and uncertain candidates. Leveraging a dynamic Anchor Tokens Cache, we introduce two complementary mechanisms: (1) Anchor-Guided Generation, which injects entropy-weighted anchor signals into masked positions to implicitly rectify attention toward the reliable global skeleton; and (2) Anchor-Perturbed Verification, which applies orthogonal perturbations to uncertain candidate tokens, destabilizing and remasking errors driven by fragile local consensus. Extensive experiments on math and coding benchmarks demonstrate that ASRD outperforms recent remasking baselines, achieving accuracy improvements of up to 6.4\% while accelerating inference throughput by up to 7.2$\times$.

</details>


### [127] [Revisiting the Systematicity in Negation in the Era of In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16867)
*Hitomi Yanaka,Taisei Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从行为系统性和表征系统性两个角度分析了LLM对否定句的理解。行为上，通过上下文学习，LLM能部分识别否定表达和范围但未达完美，且输出格式影响范围识别难度；表征上，否定线索提取的功能向量可组成，但范围识别的功能向量构建更具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定句理解一直是语言模型的挑战，即使在LLM时代也是如此，本文旨在系统性地分析LLM对否定的理解能力。

Method: 从行为系统性和表征系统性两个维度展开分析：行为层面通过演示和上下文学习测试LLM对否定表达和范围的识别；表征层面分析功能向量能否从上下文示例中稳健构建用于否定理解的任务。

Result: LLM能部分识别否定表达和范围但未达完美；否定范围识别的难度受输出格式影响；否定线索提取的功能向量可组成，但范围识别的功能向量构建更具挑战性。

Conclusion: LLM对否定的理解在行为上有限，在表征上存在不同程度的可组合性，范围识别仍是一个难点。

Abstract: Understanding the meaning of negated sentences remains one of the challenges for language models, even in the era of large language models (LLMs). We analyze systematicity regarding LLM understanding of negation from two perspectives: behavioral systematicity and representational systematicity. For behavioral systematicity, we confirm that through demonstrations and in-context learning, LLMs can recognize negation expressions and scope within sentences to some extent, but they fail to achieve perfect performance. In particular, the difficulty of the negation scope recognition for models varies depending on the output format. For representational systematicity, we analyze the extent to which function vectors can be robustly constructed from in-context examples for tasks that are essential to understanding negation. The experiments suggest that while function vectors can be composed for negation cue extraction tasks, extracting function vectors for recognizing scope is more challenging.

</details>


### [128] [Understanding Scam Trends and Rail Paths from Reddit Self-Disclosure Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16874)
*Yangjun Zhang,Mirko Bottarelli,Mark Hooper,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过收集2023-2025年Reddit上的21,304篇自述帖子，构建了标注多阶段诈骗环节（身份、通信、平台、支付）的数据集，分析了诈骗特征的时间趋势和路径复杂性，发现诈骗过程以多环节为主、不同诈骗类型在路径复杂度上存在系统性差异，且社区支持行为随时间变得更加细致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分析了诈骗类型和环节的特征，但缺乏跨年趋势追踪，且由于缺乏包含标注和覆盖多种诈骗类型的开源数据集，对诈骗环节之间关系的研究受到阻碍。

Method: 从Reddit诈骗相关子版块收集2023-2025年21,304篇帖子，通过启发式标注进行诈骗环节（身份、通信、平台、支付）的年度趋势分析；再通过LLM辅助方法（经人工评估）标注1,800篇包含显式或可恢复诈骗链的帖子进行路径分析；最后对帖子评论运行主题模型分析社区支持行为。

Result: 诈骗过程以多环节为主；不同诈骗类型和环节组件在不同年份主导地位不同；不同诈骗类型在路径复杂度上存在系统性差异；Reddit社区支持行为随时间变得更加细致。

Conclusion: 该工作支持合成诈骗链数据模拟和AI相关诈骗风险评估，但研究结论可能无法泛化到其他平台。

Abstract: Online scam behavior is inherently multi-stage, and the lifecycle includes temporally ordered rails and events rather than isolated signals. Existing works analyze characteristics of scam types and rails, but they do not track scam trends across years. Moreover, the work on the relations between rails is hampered due to the lack of open-source datasets with annotations and coverage of different scam types. To address these gaps, we build a dataset to analyze the yearly trend of scam characteristics and rail paths using Reddit self-disclosure narratives from 2023 to 2025. We collect 21,304 posts from scam-related subreddits with at least one rail among identity, communication, platform, and payment for trend analysis by heuristic annotation. Then, we label 1,800 posts containing explicit or recoverable scam chains by an LLM-assisted method for scam path analysis. The method is evaluated with human annotation. Lastly, we run a topic model on the comments of the posts to analyze the community support behavior. The results reveal that scam processes are predominantly multi-rail. Across years, different scam types and rail components dominate. Different scam types vary systematically in path complexity. Reddit support behaviors have become more detailed over time. This work supports synthetic scam chain data simulation and AI-related scam risk assessment, though findings may not generalise to other platforms.

</details>


### [129] [Compositional Reasoning Depth Predicts Clinical AI Failure: Empirical Evidence Consistent with Transformer Compositionality Limits in Electronic Health Record Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16890)
*Sanjay Basu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跃点计数（hop-count）作为预测大语言模型在电子健康档案问答任务中失败的系统性指标。随着所需推理步骤增加，模型准确率呈单调下降，扩展思维模式未能显著缓解此问题，跃点计数可作为跨架构的误差预测因子，对临床AI部署风险分层具有直接意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合准确率基准掩盖了大语言模型在电子健康档案问答中失败的系统性结构：需要更多推理步骤的问题会产生不成比例的更多错误。受Transformer组合性局限的理论结果启发，作者希望引入一个理论驱动的预测因子来解释和预测模型失败模式。

Method: 定义预指定的跃点计数分类法（回答临床问题所需的独立推理步骤数），标注313个MedAlign EHR问答对（4个跃点级别），在301个问题上进行模型内消融（claude-sonnet-4-6，零样本vs扩展思维）和跨架构复制（gpt-4o和gpt-5.4-2026-03-05，零样本），并进行上下文充分性审计。

Result: 所有三个模型均呈现准确率随跃点计数单调下降：Claude Sonnet零样本从30.6%(hop=1)降至17.6%(hop=4)(OR 0.72, p=0.008)；GPT-4o从37.8%降至14.7%(OR 0.58, p<0.001)；gpt-5.4从37.8%降至23.5%(OR 0.80, p=0.027)。扩展思维未显著平坦化准确率-深度曲线，思维token使用量与跃点计数相关(r=0.31, p<0.0001)。

Conclusion: 跃点计数是一个理论驱动、跨架构的大语言模型在EHR问答任务中的误差预测因子，准确率下降反映了组合推理困难而非上下文截断问题，对临床AI部署风险分层具有直接意义。

Abstract: Aggregate accuracy benchmarks conceal a systematic structure in how large language models fail at electronic health record (EHR) question answering: questions requiring more inferential steps produce disproportionately more errors. Motivated by theoretical results on transformer compositionality limits, we introduce a pre-specified hop-count taxonomy -- the number of distinct reasoning steps required to answer a clinical question from an EHR -- as a principled predictor of model failure. We annotate 313 clinician-generated MedAlign EHR question-answer pairs across four hop levels and evaluate 301 questions in a within-model ablation (claude-sonnet-4-6, zero-shot vs. extended thinking) and cross-architecture replications (gpt-4o and gpt-5.4-2026-03-05, zero-shot). All three models, spanning two providers and two OpenAI generations (GPT-4 and GPT-5), show monotone accuracy decline with hop count: Claude Sonnet zero-shot falls from 30.6% (hop=1) to 17.6% (hop=4) (Cochran-Armitage z=-2.30, p=0.011; OR per hop 0.72, 95% CI [0.56,0.92], p=0.008); GPT-4o replicates this (37.8% to 14.7%; OR 0.58 [0.45,0.75], p<0.001); and gpt-5.4-2026-03-05 confirms it (37.8% to 23.5%; OR 0.80 [0.66,0.98], p=0.027). A pre-specified context-sufficiency audit shows higher-hop questions are not differentially disadvantaged by EHR truncation (answerability 93-95% at hops 2-4 vs. 79% at hop=1), so the decline reflects compositional reasoning difficulty. Extended thinking did not significantly flatten the accuracy-depth curve across three reasoning conditions, and thinking-token usage scaled with hop count (r=0.31, p<0.0001), consistent with the predicted O(k) computational requirement. Hop count is thus a theory-motivated, cross-architecture predictor of large-language-model error on EHR question answering, with direct implications for deployment risk stratification of clinical AI.

</details>


### [130] [Contrastive-Difference CKA Reveals Concept-Specific Structural Alignment Across Language Model Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16897)
*Xueping Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 不同LLM架构在编码高层概念时表现出几何与功能解离：几何表征适度收敛，但功能可迁移性近乎完美。CKA_Delta作为一种免训练诊断方法，能有效区分概念特异性收敛与通用相似性，并在多个概念域和架构族中验证了这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不同LLM架构是否以结构兼容的方式编码高层概念，即跨架构的概念表征是否存在几何和功能上的普遍性（universality）。

Method: 提出对比差异CKA（CKA_Delta），一种免训练诊断方法，通过计算逐样本对比差异上的核对齐，分离概念特异性收敛与通用相似性，克服了标准CKA无法区分的局限。

Result: 在六个概念域中验证了几何-功能解离现象：五个域几何判别显著（p≤0.017），安全概念呈收敛功能趋势（p=0.08）；涵盖代码vs自然语言、推理vs回忆等非指令概念；Gemma被识别为架构异常值（d=1.08, AUC=0.79）。

Conclusion: CKA_Delta可作为实用的机制分类器和架构异常检测工具，用于跨架构概念监控，无需训练。观察到70B-70B模型对可能表明普适性随规模增强，但需更多大模型验证。

Abstract: Do different LLM architectures encode high-level concepts in structurally compatible ways? We systematically characterize a geometric-functional universality dissociation: across multiple concept domains and architectural families, moderate geometric convergence coexists with near-perfect functional transfer. Using contrastive-difference CKA (CKA_Delta), a training-free diagnostic that computes kernel alignment on per-sample contrastive differences, we isolate concept-specific convergence from generic similarity -- achieving significant discrimination where standard CKA cannot. The dissociation replicates across all six concept domains we test (five with p <= 0.017 geometric discrimination and safety as a converging-functional trend, p = 0.08), including two non-instruction concepts (code-vs-NL, reasoning-vs-recall) validated without system prompts; a single 70B--70B pair provides an observational note that universality may strengthen with scale, requiring replication with additional >=70B models. We position CKA_Delta as a practical regime classifier and architectural outlier detector (Gemma: d = 1.08, AUC = 0.79) rather than an absolute transfer-accuracy predictor, providing a training-free diagnostic for cross-architecture concept monitoring.

</details>


### [131] [Speaking the Language of Science: Toward a General-Purpose Generative Foundation Model for the Natural Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16905)
*Mingyang Li,Yurou Liu,Jieping Ye,Bing Su,Ji-Rong Wen,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LOGOS是一个基于共享科学语法的统一生成式语言模型，通过将科学对象及其空间交互编码为离散token序列，以单一自回归框架统一自然科学中的异构任务，在多个领域达到或超越专用基线，且模型规模与性能正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索“一个模型适用于所有自然科学”的可行性，验证科学基础模型与大规模语言模型（LLMs）在架构、训练范式和推理基础设施上深度对齐的路径，避免为AI4S构建独立的技术栈。

Method: 将科学对象和空间接触/约束模式编码为离散token序列，构建共享科学语法；采用自回归框架，将下游任务统一表述为同一语法空间中的下一个token预测；训练1B、3B、8B三种规模的模型。

Result: LOGOS在多种任务上匹配或超越领域专用基线；模型参数规模（1B/3B/8B）与性能呈一致正相关。

Conclusion: AI4S的未来可能不在于构建独立的科学技术栈，而在于通过共享架构、训练范式和推理基础设施，将科学基础模型与LLMs深度对齐，使LLMs成为AI4S的新入口。

Abstract: In this report, we present LOGOS (Language Of Generative Objects in Science), a scientific generative language model that unifies heterogeneous tasks across the natural sciences within a single autoregressive framework based on a shared scientific grammar. It encodes diverse scientific objects and their spatial interactions as token sequences over a common vocabulary. By representing spatial contact and constraint patterns as discrete tokens, the model captures complex structural interactions in a purely sequential manner, without relying on explicit coordinates or geometric neural networks. This unified representation enables a wide range of downstream tasks to be formulated consistently as next-token prediction in the same grammar space, creating strong alignment between continued multi-domain pre-training and downstream objectives. Across diverse tasks, LOGOS consistently matches or outperforms domain-specific baselines, providing preliminary evidence for the feasibility of "one model fits all" in the natural sciences. We train LOGOS models at different scales (1B, 3B, and 8B parameters) and find a consistent positive correlation between model size and performance. This suggests that the future of AI for Science (AI4S) may not lie in building an independent technical stack that is separated from large language models (LLMs). Instead, it may depend on deeply aligning scientific foundation models with LLMs through shared architectures, shared training paradigms, and shared inference infrastructure, so that LLMs can truly become a new entry point for AI4S. We release the model weights and associated resources to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [132] [LESS Is More: Mutual-Stability Sampling for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16908)
*Amr Mohamed,Guokan Shang,Michalis Vazirgiannis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LESS是一种无需训练、模型无关的自适应采样器，通过互稳定性规则决定何时将掩码位置转换为可见token，在保持或提升准确率的同时，将反向去噪步骤减少72.1%，从而降低推理延迟和计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）在固定步数采样中效率低下：已稳定的位置浪费计算资源，而不稳定的位置可能过早被确定。现有方法缺乏自适应地决定何时提交token的能力。

Method: LESS将token承诺建模为在线停止问题，提出互稳定性采样规则：当（1）top-1预测置信度高，（2）top-1 token在最近反向步骤中持续不变，（3）预测分布在top-K步间Jensen-Shannon散度稳定时，才允许该位置被解掩。

Result: 在Dream-7B、LLaDA-8B等模型上，LESS在7个基准测试中优于现有训练无关自适应采样器，同时比固定预算解码减少72.1%的反向步骤，转化为更低的延迟和推理计算量。

Conclusion: LESS通过自适应步数分配有效解决了dLLMs固定采样效率低的问题，是一种实用且高效的推理加速方法。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive decoding by iteratively refining masked sequences, enabling parallel token updates and bidirectional conditioning. Their practical efficiency, however, is limited by sampling procedures that execute a fixed number of reverse denoising steps selected before decoding, spending computation on already-stable positions and sometimes committing unstable ones too early. We present \textsc{LESS}, a training-free, model-agnostic adaptive sampler that treats token commitment as an online stopping problem. \textsc{LESS} implements mutual-stability sampling through a joint stability rule that makes a masked position eligible for unmasking only when its top-1 prediction has high confidence, its top-1 token persists across recent reverse steps, and its predictive distribution is stable under top-$K$ inter-step Jensen--Shannon divergence. We evaluate \textsc{LESS} on Dream-7B, LLaDA-8B, and LLaDA-1.5-8B, covering full-sequence diffusion and semi-autoregressive blockwise sampling regimes, across seven benchmarks spanning general knowledge, math, and code. \textsc{LESS} improves average accuracy over strong training-free adaptive samplers while using $72.1\%$ fewer reverse steps than fixed-budget decoding. Since each reverse step requires a Transformer forward pass, these step-count reductions translate into fewer forward evaluations, lower measured wall-clock latency, and lower estimated inference compute.

</details>


### [133] [IMPACTeen: Intentions, Manipulation, Persuasion, Annotations, and Consequences in Teen Communication Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16910)
*Aleksander Szczęsny,Wiktoria Mieleszczenko-Kowszewicz,Maciej Markiewicz,Beata Bajcar,Tomasz Adamczyk,Jolanta Babiak,Grzegorz Chodak,Przemysław Kazienko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IMPACTeen是一个包含1,021段文本、5,100条独立标注记录的青年社会影响力场景数据集，涵盖人际、媒体和数字环境。文本从青少年、家长、心理学家、传播专家和教师五个视角进行标注，覆盖影响力存在、技术、意图、后果、抵抗、反应和标注置信度等多维度信息。数据集通过受控LLM生成和两阶段人工编辑验证构建，以波兰语创建并附带英文版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会影响力检测数据集缺乏针对青少年情境的多视角标注资源，且未充分考虑标注者分歧和跨语言建模需求。作者旨在构建一个高质量、多维度、多视角标注的青年社会影响力数据集，以支持相关计算语言学和社会科学研究。

Method: 采用受控LLM生成初始文本，经过两阶段人工编辑和验证确保青年情境的真实性；从五个不同视角（青少年、家长、心理学家、传播专家、教师）进行多维度标注，涵盖影响力存在、技术、意图、后果、抵抗、反应和标注置信度。

Result: 构建了1,021段文本、5,100条独立标注记录的IMPACTeen数据集，以波兰语创建并附带英文版本，支持社会影响力检测、标注者分歧分析、跨语言建模等研究。

Conclusion: 该数据集为青少年社会影响力检测提供了独特的多视角资源，有助于理解不同群体对社会影响力感知的差异，并支持多语言场景下的模型训练与评估。

Abstract: IMPACTeen is a dataset of textual social influence scenarios spanning interpersonal, media-based, and digital settings in an adolescent context. It contains 1,021 texts, 5,100 individual annotation records, and gold labels for social influence techniques, with each text annotated from five distinct perspectives: teenagers, parents, psychologists, communication experts, and teachers. The resource was constructed through constrained LLM generation, followed by a two-step human editing and validation phase aimed at ensuring youth-context realism. A multi-dimensional annotation covered influence presence, techniques, intentions, consequences, resistance, reactions, and annotation confidence. The dataset supports research on social influence detection, annotator disagreement, cross-lingual modeling, and the training and evaluation of language models. The dataset was created in Polish and is accompanied by a corresponding English version.

</details>


### [134] [Exploring Extrinsic and Intrinsic Properties for Effective Reasoning with Code Interpreter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16934)
*Patomporn Payoungkhamdee,Napat Laosaengpha,Jenta Wonglertsakul,Pittawat Taveekitworachai,Pume Tuchinda,Panjapong Poobanchuen,Ekapol Chuangsuwanich,Can Udomcharoenchaikit,Samuel Cahyawijaya,Peerat Limkonchotiwat,Sarana Nutanong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了代码解释器（CI）推理中有效行为的两种属性：外在属性（关键token）和内在属性（代码特定认知行为），发现更强的CI推理模型更频繁地展现出验证、回溯和后向链等行为，并探索了在推理和训练阶段利用这些属性的方法与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码解释器推理已被证明能增强LLM的推理能力，但其有效推理的行为属性尚未被充分探索。本文旨在从自然语言推理研究中借鉴的外在属性（关键token）和内在属性（代码特定认知行为）两个视角，系统表征CI推理的有效行为特征。

Method: 跨多个LLM分析CI推理模型的外在属性（关键token）和内在属性（代码特定认知行为）；在推理阶段通过追加代码特定关键token提升性能；在训练阶段通过引入代码特定认知行为增强监督微调和强化学习。

Result: 更强的CI推理模型一致表现出更高的关键token频率和认知行为（验证、回溯、后向链）；推理时追加关键token在数学、排序、优化任务上有提升；训练时引入认知行为在两个模型上提升了SFT和RL性能，同时减少了过度思考和提高了token效率，但也揭示了某些模型增益受限的因素。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统表征了CI有效推理的行为属性，展示了利用关键属性改进CI推理的潜力与局限性。

Abstract: Reasoning with a Code Interpreter (CI) has emerged as an effective paradigm for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) through executable computation and iterative verification. Despite its growing adoption, the behavioral properties underlying effective code reasoning remain largely underexplored. In this work, we investigate code reasoning from two distinct perspectives inspired by prior studies of natural language reasoning: extrinsic properties, represented by crucial tokens, and intrinsic properties, represented by code-specific cognitive behaviors. Across multiple LLMs, we find that stronger CI reasoning models consistently exhibit a higher prevalence of crucial tokens and cognitive behaviors, particularly verification, backtracking, and backward chaining. Building on these observations, we examine how these properties can be leveraged during both inference and training. At inference time, appending code-specific crucial tokens improves performance on several reasoning capabilities, including mathematical, ordering, and optimization, while yielding limited benefits elsewhere. At training time, augmenting a state-of-the-art framework with code-specific cognitive behaviors improves supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning performance in two of three evaluated models. Further analysis shows that these behaviors reduce overthinking in incorrect responses and improve token efficiency, while also revealing factors that limit gains in a certain model. Our findings provide the first systematic characterization of effective reasoning with CI and demonstrate both the potential and limitations of leveraging key properties to improve CI-based reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [A Definition of Good Explanations and the Challenges Explaining LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14838)
*Louis Mahon,Elliot Ford,Callum Hackett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从反事实解释出发，提出一个好的解释需要考虑对话者对各事实的先验信念，并探讨了该定义对AI可解释性的影响，特别是为何LLM输出难以产生好的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 好的解释的定义长期以来是哲学争论的话题，在AI输出背景下这一问题重新受到关注。可解释性对AI在许多场景中的采用至关重要，但要想产生AI系统的好解释，首先必须理解什么是好的解释。

Method: 受反事实解释概念的启发，提出在定义解释时还需考虑对话者对解释中各事实的先验信念。

Result: 探讨了该定义对AI可解释性的影响，并分析了为什么LLM输出难以产生好的解释。

Conclusion: 一个好的解释不仅需要反事实信息，还必须考虑接收者的先验信念；LLM输出因其特性难以满足这一标准。

Abstract: How to define a good explanation is a long-standing philosophical debate which has found recent renewed interest in the context of AI outputs. Explainability is crucial for AI adoption in many contexts, but in order to produce good explanations of AI systems, we must first have an understanding of what good explanations are. In this paper we propose a definition inspired by the notion of counterfactual explanations, however we argue that one must also take into account the interlocutor's prior beliefs in each fact that could be offered in an explanation. We explore the ramifications of this definition for AI explainability and, in particular, why LLM outputs are difficult to produce good explanations for.

</details>


### [136] [Dr-DCI: Scaling Direct Corpus Interaction via Dynamic Workspace Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14885)
*Yi Lu,Zhuofeng Li,Ping Nie,Haoxiang Zhang,Yuyu Zhang,Kai Zou,Wenhu Chen,Jimmy Lin,Dongfu Jiang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DR-DCI提出了一种检索器引导的直接语料交互框架，通过将检索作为智能体可调用的动作来动态扩展工作空间，在保持可扩展性的同时实现了高精度证据解析，在多个基准测试中显著优于原始DCI和BM25等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索器（如BM25、ColBERT）虽然能有效排序相关文档，但仅返回排名结果或受限文档视图，限制了智能体重组材料和跨文档验证约束的能力。直接语料交互（DCI）虽允许灵活搜索、过滤、比较和验证，但随着语料库增大，全语料终端命令变得缓慢且不稳定，性能下降。

Method: DR-DCI将检索作为智能体可调用的动作，智能体动态地将相关文档拉入一个不断演化的局部工作空间，并在该工作空间内执行DCI操作。这样结合了检索器的可扩展性和DCI的局部精确操作能力。

Result: 在Browsecomp-Plus上达到71.2%准确率，比原始DCI和消融变体提升高达8.3个百分点，同时减少了工具使用、墙时间和预估成本。带工作空间保留的上下文重置将准确率进一步提升至73.3%。在10万到1000万文档的语料规模实验中保持有效，而原始DCI变得不稳定，BM25性能显著下降。在2000万规模的Wiki-18 QA中平均得分63.0，优于检索式和训练式搜索智能体基线。

Conclusion: DR-DCI通过检索器引导的动态工作空间设计，有效解决了大规模语料下直接语料交互的可扩展性问题，同时保持了DCI风格的精确操作能力。消融分析表明，排序预览和文档间DCI是性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Agentic search over large corpora relies on retriever-mediated interfaces (e.g., BM25 or ColBERT) for scalable candidate discovery. While effective at ranking relevant documents, these interfaces expose evidence only as ranked results or bounded document views, limiting agents' ability to reorganize material and verify constraints across documents. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) addresses this limitation by exposing shell-executable corpus operations for flexible search, filtering, comparison, and verification. However, full-corpus terminal commands become slow and unstable as the corpus grows, degrading performance and efficiency. We introduce DR-DCI, a retriever-steered DCI framework that treats retrieval as an agent-callable action for expanding a local workspace. Rather than operating directly over the full corpus, the agent dynamically pulls relevant documents into an evolving workspace and conducts DCI operations within it. This design combines retriever-level recall with DCI-style precision: retrieval keeps exploration scalable, while DCI preserves the local operations needed for effective evidence resolution. Experiments show that DR-DCI is both effective and efficient across scales. On Browsecomp-Plus, DR-DCI reaches 71.2\% accuracy, improving over raw DCI and ablated variants by up to 8.3 points while reducing tool usage, wall time, and estimated cost. With workspace-preserving context reset, accuracy further improves to 73.3\%. In corpus-scaling experiments, DR-DCI remains effective from 100K to 10M documents, whereas raw DCI becomes unstable and BM25 performs substantially worse. DR-DCI also scales to a 20M-scale file-per-document Wiki-18 QA setting, achieving an average score of 63.0 across six benchmarks and outperforming retrieval-based and trained search-agent baselines. Ablation analysis further shows that ranked previews and inter-document DCI are key to performance.

</details>


### [137] [Relational Structural Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14892)
*Adiba Ejaz,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系结构因果模型（RSCM），将结构因果模型扩展到对象和关系可变的场景，通过关系因果图和符号识别准则实现因果、反事实和组合泛化查询的识别，并设计了关系神经因果模型，在交通场景模拟中优于非关系基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能系统需要同时具备因果建模能力（支持干预和反事实推理）和组合泛化能力（推广到未见过的对象组合），现有结构因果模型（SCM）假设固定的变量集合，无法处理对象及其关系动态变化的情境。

Method: 首先定义关系结构因果模型（RSCM），扩展SCM以处理可变对象和关系；然后定义关系因果图，推导符号识别准则，解决在存在未观测混杂时的因果和观测查询识别问题；最后提出关系神经因果模型（RNCM），一种可证明正确的实现方法。

Result: 在模拟交通场景（包含变化的车辆、信号灯和行人）中，关系神经因果模型在因果推理任务上显著优于非关系基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入关系结构因果模型及相应的识别理论，在理论上定义了何时以及如何学习具备因果性和组合泛化能力的环境模型，并在实践中验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: An artificial intelligence must have a model of its environment that is causal, supporting reasoning about interventions and counterfactuals, and also combinatorial, supporting generalization to unseen combinations of objects. In this work, we formally study when and how such a model can be learned. We develop relational structural causal models, extending structural causal models (Pearl 2009) to settings where objects and their relations vary. First, we show how answers to not only causal but also observational queries about unseen combinations of objects can not be identified without further assumptions. To enable such identification--including in the presence of unobserved confounding--we define relational causal graphs and derive symbolic identification criteria. Finally, we propose relational neural causal models, a provably correct approach that outperforms non-relational baselines on simulated traffic scenes with varying cars, signals, and pedestrians.

</details>


### [138] [Trust Between AI Agents: Measuring Formation, Breakage, and Recovery, with Implications for Governing Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14923)
*Yujiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于代价验证的行为测量方法来衡量AI智能体之间的信任程度，发现不同模型在信任形成、破裂和恢复上存在显著差异，且过度验证与决策效率低下相关，而非安全性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏标准化方法来衡量AI智能体之间的信任，而随着语言模型智能体越来越多地以团队形式协作，每个智能体需要判断对队友的信任程度，因此需要一个可观察、可量化的信任度量标准。

Method: 在合作生存游戏中，通过验证队友工作消耗资源（代价验证）与信任错误答案可能致命之间的权衡，以相对于无记忆版本的验证减少量作为信任的行为测量指标，研究6个前沿模型快照的信任形成、破裂和恢复。

Result: 四个较大模型（Claude Opus 4.6、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.1、Gemini 3.1 Pro）在可靠队友下验证减少60-85%，两个较小模型几乎没有调整；信任破裂后模型反应各异（集中怀疑 culprit 或对整个团队更谨慎）；恢复速度慢于形成速度，且聚类失败比分散失败导致更持久的怀疑。

Conclusion: 信任倾向可在部署前测量，多智能体AI系统治理的核心关注点应是校准（calibration）而非最大怀疑（maximal suspicion），因为过度验证与决策犹豫相关而非安全性提升。

Abstract: As language-model agents increasingly work in teams, each agent must decide how much to trust its teammates. Yet we lack a standard way to measure trust between AI agents. We propose a behavioral measure based on costly verification. In a cooperative survival game, checking a teammate's work consumes resources, while trusting a wrong answer can be fatal. Relative to a memoryless version of the same model, reduced verification provides an observable measure of trust. Using this framework, we study trust formation, breakage, and recovery across six frontier model snapshots. When paired with a consistently reliable teammate, four snapshots (Claude Opus 4.6, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.1, and Gemini 3.1 Pro) reduce verification by roughly 60-85%, whereas two smaller snapshots show little or no such adjustment. Failures reverse this discount, but models differ in how they respond. Some concentrate renewed scrutiny on the culprit, while others become more cautious toward the entire team. Recovery is slower than formation, and clustered failures sustain suspicion far longer than the same number of failures spread apart. These differences have practical consequences. Models that form trust verify less, decide more quickly, and achieve higher payoffs in our environment. By contrast, persistent over-verification is associated with indecision rather than safety. Our results show that trust dispositions can be measured before deployment and suggest that calibration, rather than maximal suspicion, should be the central concern in the governance of multi-agent AI systems.

</details>


### [139] [PrologMCP: A Standardized Prolog Tool Interface for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14935)
*Agnieszka Mensfelt,Adarsh Prabhakaran,Adrian Haret,Vince Trencsenyi,Kostas Stathis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PrologMCP，一个通过模型上下文协议将 Prolog 暴露为有状态工具的通用服务器。相比增强内部推理的 LLM，将推理委托给 Prolog 的形式化智能体在 PARARULE-Plus 数据集上表现更好，在困难子集上准确率接近完美（1.00/0.99），而推理型 LLM 降至 0.95/0.94。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿推理型语言模型在深度演绎推理任务上仍然失败，且通过扩展内部推理来提升性能的成本高昂。符号委托提供了一种补充途径，但当前的自动形式化流程通常是针对特定任务或代理的定制集成，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出 PrologMCP，一个任务无关的开源服务器，通过模型上下文协议（MCP）将 Prolog 暴露为有状态工具。提供紧凑的工具接口、结构化错误报告和每会话隔离，使“翻译-运行-检查-修复”循环成为 MCP 兼容代理的可复用原语。评估使用 PrologMCP 增强的形式化智能体与标准及推理型 LLM 在 PARARULE-Plus 数据集上的表现。

Result: 在通用样本上，形式化智能体匹配或超越推理型 LLM（准确率 1.00 vs 1.00/0.998），最大提升相对于标准模型（GPT-4.1 为 0.762）。在困难子集上，形式化智能体保持接近完美（1.00/0.99），而推理型 LLM 降至 0.95/0.94。

Conclusion: 通过 MCP 将推理委托给 Prolog 是一种稳健且可检查的替代方案，优于扩展的自然语言推理方法。

Abstract: Frontier reasoning-tuned language models still fail on deductive tasks at depth, and the cost of improved performance through extended internal reasoning scales poorly. Symbolic delegation offers a complementary route: a language model translates the problem, while a solver performs the inference. However, current autoformalization pipelines for logic programming are typically bespoke integrations tied to particular tasks or agents. We introduce PrologMCP, a task-agnostic, open-source server that exposes Prolog as a stateful tool through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Its compact tool interface, structured error reporting, and per-session isolation make the translate-run-inspect-repair loop a reusable primitive for MCP-capable agents. We evaluate a formalizer agent enhanced with PrologMCP against standard and reasoning LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-4.1, and o4-mini) on two subsets of PARARULE-Plus: a general-purpose sample and a more challenging one targeting a specific failure mode of natural-language reasoning. On the general sample, the formalizer matches or exceeds reasoning LLMs (accuracy 1.00 vs.\ 1.00 / 0.998), with the largest gains over standard models (0.762 for GPT-4.1). On the challenging subset, the formalizer remains near-perfect (1.00 / 0.99) while reasoning LLMs drop to 0.95 / 0.94. These results suggest that delegating inference to Prolog via MCP is a robust and inspectable alternative to extended natural-language reasoning.

</details>


### [140] [Semantics-Enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14941)
*Shiqiao Zhou,Zipeng Wu,Holger Schöner,Edouard Fouché,IAG Wilson,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SERAF框架，通过双检索（时间序列相似性+自生成文本描述）增强非平稳时间序列预测，在7个数据集上优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索增强的时间序列预测方法仅依赖时间序列相似性进行检索，在非平稳条件下效果不足，需要引入语义信息来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 构建双检索机制：(1) 基于时间序列相似度的检索；(2) 基于自生成文本描述的语义检索。两类检索结果被选择性联合用于引导未来预测。

Result: 在7个真实数据集上，SERAF优于主流基线方法，验证了融合数值相似性与语义信息的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合时间序列的数值视图和语义视图可以更有效地应对非平稳性，为检索增强的时间序列预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models often benefit from historical patterns. Inspired by Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), recent research explored retrieving relevant historical time series segments to enhance forecasting. However, relying solely on time series similarity is often insufficient for retrieval under non-stationarity. To address this, we propose a multimodal approach: a \textbf{S}emantics-\textbf{E}nhanced \textbf{R}etrieval-\textbf{A}ugmented Time Series \textbf{F}orecasting framework, SERAF. Unlike mainstream approaches that depend only on time series similarity, SERAF conducts dual retrieval over the time series and their self-generated textual descriptions. It retrieves two complementary sets of historical patterns and corresponding futures, which are selectively and jointly used to guide future predictions. Experiments across seven real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SERAF in bridging numerical and semantic views of time series compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [141] [AI Engram: In Search of Memory Traces in Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14997)
*Jea Kwon,Dong-Kyum Kim,Jiwon Kim,Yonghyun Kim,Woong Kook,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出几何框架识别深度神经网络中的"AI印记"，通过将神经科学标准形式化为约束逆问题，推导出闭合形式估计器，实现了对学习记忆的线性编辑（组合/擦除），无需迭代优化，从MLP到LLM的实验验证了其因果有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究深度神经网络是否保留类似生物记忆单元的可识别记忆痕迹，桥接生物记忆理论与人工表征学习，并实现记忆的精确定位与操控。

Method: 将神经科学的特异性、再激活、充分性和必要性标准形式化为约束逆问题，在参数流形上推导出闭合形式的自然梯度更新估计器，从而从全局纠缠参数中分离出单个记忆痕迹。

Result: AI印记支持通过线性算术对任意子集记忆进行外科手术式的组合或擦除，无需迭代优化；从简单MLP到LLM的实验验证了其因果有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作桥接了生物记忆理论与人工表征学习，揭示了深度网络在分布式存储中同时支持功能特异性的几何机制。

Abstract: Memory formation is fundamental to intelligence, yet whether deep neural networks preserve identifiable memory traces analogous to biological memory units remains an open question. This work introduces a geometric framework to identify such "AI engrams" by formalizing the neuroscientific criteria of specificity, reactivation, sufficiency, and necessity into a constrained inverse problem. We derive a closed-form estimator that isolates individual memory traces from globally entangled parameters, and show that this biologically-derived solution corresponds to a natural gradient update on the parameter manifold. AI engrams enable surgical manipulation of learned knowledge: any subset of memories can be composed or erased through linear arithmetic, without iterative optimization. Experiments ranging from simple MLPs to LLMs demonstrate the causal validity and substantial scalability of AI engrams. Together, these results bridge theories of biological memory and artificial representation learning and offer geometric insight into how deep networks simultaneously support functional specificity within distributed storage.

</details>


### [142] [Metric Match: A Subset Selection Approach to Evaluating LLM Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15029)
*Alyssa Unell,Natalie Dullerud,Naomi Boneh,Meena Jagadeesan,Tatsu Hashimoto,Nigam Shah,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Metric Match方法，通过选择少量样本进行人工标注来估计LLM评估者与人类评分者之间的相关性可靠性指标，相比随机选择在4种相关性指标和15个数据集上达到0.838胜率，平均估计误差降低18.7%，注释需求减少32.5%，并在医学案例研究中节省1,041.67美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估器在替代人工评估开放文本生成时，其可靠性与人类评分者的一致性高度依赖昂贵的人工标注。现有方法需要大量人工标注来评估这种一致性，成本过高，因此需要一种能从有限标注中准确估计LLM评估器可靠性指标的方法。

Method: 提出Metric Match方法：从数据集中选择一个子集进行人工标注，使得该子集在获取的合成标签上与总体可靠性指标相匹配，从而用少量标注样本估计基于相关性的可靠性指标。

Result: 在4种相关性指标和15个数据集上，Metric Match相比随机子集选择的胜率为0.838，平均估计误差降低18.7%，注释需求减少32.5%。在医学案例研究中节省1,041.67美元专家注释费用。在可靠性分类任务（判断评估器是否超过部署阈值）上也优于随机选择。

Conclusion: Metric Match能有效从有限标注中估计LLM评估器的可靠性，显著降低人工标注成本，并提供开源代码和可安装包，具有实用价值。

Abstract: LLM judges are used to reduce the need for costly human labor in evaluating open-ended text generation. However, the reliability of these judges depends critically on their alignment with human raters -- a property that itself depends on costly human annotations. In this work, we develop a method (Metric Match) for estimating correlation-based reliability metrics of LLM judges from limited annotations. Metric Match selects a subset of samples for human annotation such that the subset matches the population reliability metric with respect to acquired synthetic labels. We empirically show that Metric Match achieves a win-rate of 0.838 against random subset selection across four different correlation metrics and 15 datasets, with an 18.7% decrease in average estimation error and reduces annotation needs by 32.5%. We provide a cost model and highlight a medical case study where our method saves $1,041.67 compared to random selection for expert annotation. Further, we shift our task from reliability estimation to reliability classification of whether a given judge is above a deployment threshold, outperforming random selection with Metric Match. All project code is publicly available, and we additionally provide an installable package for ease of use.

</details>


### [143] [OSGuard: A Benchmark for Safety in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15034)
*Mina Mohammadmirzaei,Jeffrey Flanigan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OSGuard基准测试，通过动作级基准和风险增强执行套件双粒度评估计算机使用智能体的安全性，揭示当前多模态护栏在局部判断与端到端安全之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体评估仅关注任务成功率，忽略了智能体通过不安全捷径达成目标的安全隐患。

Method: 构建双粒度基准OSGuard：1) 动作级基准，对上下文中的提议动作标注为允许、无关或不安全；2) 风险增强执行套件，在保留原始任务目标的同时修改环境引入潜在风险，并添加状态安全不变量以区分安全与不安全的任务完成。

Result: 当前多模态护栏在独立动作判断上表现良好，但风险增强的执行套件揭示了局部监督与可靠端到端安全之间仍存在差距。

Conclusion: 双粒度设计能够更精确地诊断模型是否既能识别不安全动作，又能在作为护栏部署时提升整体任务安全性。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are increasingly evaluated by whether they complete realistic desktop and web tasks. However, task success alone can miss failures in which an agent reaches the nominal goal through an unsafe shortcut. We introduce OSGuard, a dual-granularity benchmark suite for evaluating safety in computer-use agents under benign, unchanged user instructions. OSGuard contains an action-level benchmark for local guardrail decisions and a risk-augmented execution suite for end-to-end evaluation. The action-level benchmark consists of contextualized proposed actions labeled as allowed, unrelated, or unsafe, each judged relative to the original instruction and current interface state. The execution suite contains manually constructed OSWorld-derived task variants in which the original task remains achievable, but the environment is modified to introduce latent hazards such as destructive overwrites, etc. Each variant is paired with augmented evaluators that retain the original task-success criterion while adding explicit state-based safety invariants, allowing us to distinguish safe completions from unsafe completions that satisfy the nominal task objective. Our experimental results on OSGuard show that current multimodal guardrails can perform well on isolated action judgments, while risk-augmented execution exposes remaining gaps between local oversight and reliable end-to-end safety. This dual-granularity design enables more precise diagnosis of whether models can both recognize unsafe proposed actions and improve full-task safety when deployed as guardrails.

</details>


### [144] [Fusion is not one-size-fits-all: Cross-Modal Representation Alignment for Time-to-Event Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15038)
*Zhemin Zhang,Weijie Chen,David Le,Amara Tariq,Alex Wallace,Matthew Stib,Juan Maria Farina,Chadi Ayoub,Reza Arsanjani,Imon Banerjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于基础模型的多模态临床数据时间-事件预测框架，通过四种融合策略对齐CT影像和纵向EHR数据，在肺栓塞死亡率和心血管疾病两大任务、多机构队列中验证了融合策略的有效性，并首次系统分析了模态不平衡下的融合行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床数据的时间-事件预测面临模态不平衡和分布偏移两大挑战，现有方法缺乏通用且可跨机构推广的对齐框架。

Method: 分别用领域专用基础模型独立编码CT和EHR模态，通过四种融合策略（晚期融合、对比对齐、交叉注意力、共注意力）在共享隐空间中对齐表征，在肺栓塞死亡率和心血管疾病两大任务的多机构队列上评估。

Result: 融合策略在模态贡献相当时一致性指数提升1.5-5.4%；对比融合（尤其CLMBR表征）效果最稳定；不同任务和场景下最优融合策略不同（MACE内部分支交叉注意力最优、外部图像引导共注意力最优）。

Conclusion: 任务感知的多模态对齐是鲁棒泛化和可扩展临床部署的必要设计原则，本文首次系统分析了模态不平衡下的融合行为。

Abstract: Accurate time-to-event (TTE) prediction from multimodal clinical data remains challenging due to modality imbalance and distribution shift. We introduce a foundation model-driven framework for cross-modal representation alignment between CT imaging and longitudinal EHR data, designed to generalize across tasks and institutions. CT and EHR modalities are encoded independently using domain-specific foundation models and aligned in a shared latent space through four principled fusion strategies: late fusion, contrastive alignment, cross-attention, and co-attention. We evaluate two clinically distinct TTE tasks: pulmonary embolism (PE) mortality and cardiovascular disease (CVD) outcomes, on large-scale multi-institutional cohorts (PE: N=3,099 train; 1,098 internal; 435 external; CVD: N=2,951 train; 837 internal; 682 external). Fusion consistently improves concordance index by 1.5-5.4% over unimodal baselines when modalities contribute comparably. Overall, contrastive multimodal fusion, particularly with CLMBR representations, provided the most consistent and statistically robust improvements, especially for PE mortality prediction. For MACE, cross-attention (one-hot) achieved the highest internal performance and image-guided co-attention achieved the best external performance. We therefore introduce a generalizable foundation model-based cross-modal alignment framework and provide the first systematic analysis of fusion behavior under modality imbalance in TTE prediction. Our results establish task-aware multimodal alignment as a necessary design principle for robust generalization and scalable clinical deployment.

</details>


### [145] [Risk-Aware LLM Agents for Geospatial Data Retrieval: Design and Preliminary Adversarial Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15077)
*Kyle Gao,Joel Cumming,Jonathan Li,Linlin Xu,David A. Clausi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型的智能框架，通过自然语言查询将用户意图转换为结构化API调用，实现从云地理空间目录中高效检索遥感数据。系统集成了Guardrail（安全与策略）、General-QA（意图理解）和Recommender-Analyst（API调用生成）三个智能体，采用模块化设计可跨平台移植，支持环境监测、灾害响应和气候分析等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感数据检索需要用户掌握特定API语法和平台结构，缺乏自然语言交互接口，限制了非专业用户对海量地理空间数据的便捷访问；同时，多轮对抗性交互场景下存在安全漏洞和高影响失败风险。

Method: 构建三智能体协同架构：Guardrail负责安全策略和拦截防御，General-QA负责自然语言意图解析，Recommender-Analyst基于数据模式生成API调用。采用提示级安全指令与拦截级守护机制相结合的防御策略，通过API模式替换实现跨平台可移植性。

Result: 初步实验表明提示级安全指令在多轮对抗设置下可提升鲁棒性，但API操控场景中仍存在罕见的高影响失败案例；验证了拦截级Guardrail智能体的必要性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了用户意图与地理空间基础设施间的可扩展接口，实现了自动化地球观测工作流；但需要在安全性、可用性和成本效率间寻求平衡，自适应系统级防御是未来改进方向。

Abstract: We present an LLM-driven framework for retrieving remote sensing data from cloud-based geospatial catalogues using natural language queries. The system converts user intent into structured API calls, enabling efficient access to satellite imagery and environmental datasets. The architecture integrates three agents: Guardrail for safety and policy enforcement, General-QA for intent interpretation, and Recommender-Analyst for schema-aware API call generation. This coordinated design ensures reliable, semantically aligned interaction with external data services. The modular framework is portable across platforms through API schema substitution and supports applications in environmental monitoring, disaster response, and climate analysis. It establishes a scalable interface between user intent and geospatial infrastructure, enabling streamlined and automated Earth observation workflows. Preliminary experiments under adversarial multi-turn settings show that prompt-level safety instructions improve robustness, although rare high-impact failures persist in API manipulation scenarios and highlight the need for adaptive, system-level defenses that balance safety, usability, and cost efficiency, which motivates the use of our intercept-level Guardrail agent.

</details>


### [146] [Cognitive Debt: AI as Intellectual Leverage and the Dynamics of Systemic Fragility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15078)
*Shuchen Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立了认知债务的形式化理论，将AI替代而非补充第一性原理认知所产生的未验证推理义务存量建模为"认知债务"。模型包含认知资本和认知债务两个状态变量，证明理性动因会主动积累认知债务，并导致认知明斯基时刻、虚假修正循环、过度替代采用等一系列系统性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何理性个体在明知风险的情况下仍倾向于用AI替代自身思考，以及这种替代行为如何累积系统性脆弱性。

Method: 构建包含认知资本和认知债务两个状态变量的形式化数学模型，采用乘性生产技术，将认知资本作为决定AI采用回报的抵押品，通过六条命题进行理论推导。

Result: 证明了六条命题：理性动因主动积累认知债务；平静期降低风险感知并提高替代强度；认知明斯基时刻出现；危机损失呈凸性；虚假修正循环存在；分散均衡过度替代。

Conclusion: 揭示了AI替代性使用造成的系统性风险机制，指出分散均衡下存在过度采用替代性AI的社会次优问题。

Abstract: We develop a formal theory of cognitive debt: the stock of unverified reasoning obligations that accumulates when individuals use AI as a substitute rather than a complement for first-principles cognition. The model features two state variables per agent, cognitive capital and cognitive debt, and a multiplicative production technology in which cognitive capital functions as collateral that determines the return to AI adoption. We establish six propositions. Rational agents incur positive cognitive debt because the costs are deferred, partially external, and masked by short-run productivity gains. Tranquil periods lower subjective risk assessments, raise AI substitution intensity, and compound leverage, generating a cognitive Minsky moment in which subjective risk falls while true systemic fragility rises. Expected crisis losses are convex in aggregate leverage. Post-crisis, output-target pressure can produce a false-correction loop in which agents patch AI failures with more AI. The decentralised equilibrium over-adopts substitutive AI relative to the social optimum because of systemic risk, cognitive public goods, and arms-race externalities. In a two-type heterogeneous-agent economy, high-cognitive-capital agents adopt AI more intensively and may eventually erode their unaided cognitive capital below that of initially lower-skilled agents.

</details>


### [147] [VGPT-RSI for RH-Adjacent Formal Progress: Boundary Certificates, Verified Finite Lagarias Inequalities, and Explicit Failure Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15096)
*Zhixin Hu,Tao Xu,Xiaodian Sun,Li Jin,Momiao Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文不声称证明黎曼假设，而是利用VGPT-RSI系统对其进行了两个可验证的、AI辅助的形式化部分进展：构造了一个有限RH边界证书（在Rocq/CoqInterval中验证）以及一个基于Lagarias准则的形式化有限证书。系统明确识别了剩余数学障碍，如Lagarias等价性的完全形式化、全局尾部定理的证明以及超大数反例的排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼假设是数学核心未解难题，直接证明难以企及。本文动机在于探索AI辅助推理系统能否在不解谜的情况下，产生可验证的、形式化检查过的部分进展，并诚实标识剩下的数学瓶颈。

Method: 应用VGPT-RSI（可验证增长物理变换器与递归自我改进）系统。方法包括：1) 构建有限RH边界证书，使用向外舍入区间算术和Arb/FLINT球算术审计数值边界曲线，然后在Rocq/CoqInterval中形式化验证参数化安全下界不等式；2) 基于Lagarias准则（RH等价于全局不等式），形式化有限量并在Coq中生成有限证书。

Result: 成功构造了两个AI辅助的形式化证书：一个RH边界证书（涉及参数化下界曲线在区域内的不等式），一个基于Lagarias准则的有限证书（在Coq中检查通过）。系统明确识别出三个未解决的数学瓶颈：Lagarias等价性的完全形式化、超越任何有限截断的全局尾部定理的证明、可能的反例缩减至超大数或相关极值整数。

Conclusion: VGPT-RSI能够产生经过认证的RH邻近形式化进展，组织证明依赖关系，并在剩余障碍本质上是数学问题时避免过度声明。这表明AI辅助形式化验证在重大未解问题上可以做出诚实、可验证的贡献。

Abstract: The Riemann Hypothesis remains one of the central unsolved problems in mathematics. Rather than claiming proof, we investigate whether a verifiable AI-assisted reasoning system can produce reliable, formally checked partial progress while explicitly identifying the remaining mathematical obstructions. We apply the Verifiable Growing Physical Transformer with Recursive Self-Improvement (VGPT-RSI) to two RH-adjacent certification tasks. First, we construct and verify a finite RH-boundary certificate for inequality on a parameterized safe lower curve over a region. The numerical boundary curve is converted into a certificate-backed lower curve, audited using outward-rounded interval arithmetic and Arb/FLINT ball arithmetic, and then checked in Rocq/CoqInterval for the parameterized theorem. Second, we initiate a formal Lagarias-route certificate. Lagarias criterion states that RH is equivalent to the global inequality. We formalize the finite quantity and produce a Coq-checked finite certificate. The final system identifies the exact unresolved mathematical bottlenecks: formalizing the Lagarias equivalence, proving the global tail theorem beyond any finite cutoff, and potentially reducing counterexamples to colossally abundant or related extremal integers. These results demonstrate that VGPT-RSI can produce certified RH-adjacent formal progress, organize proof dependencies, and avoid overclaiming when the remaining obstruction is genuinely mathematical.

</details>


### [148] [Towards Verifiable Agentic Data Science: Solving Irregular TSQA Via Tool-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15107)
*Sanhorn Chen,Xiaoyang Chen,Boyu Liu,Roy Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IRTS-ToolBench 是一个包含1,700个问题、覆盖10种任务类型和13个领域的基准测试，专门用于评估大语言模型在不规则时间序列数据上的问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中的时间序列数据普遍存在不规则性（异步观测、信息性缺失、采样频率变化），但现有的时间序列问答基准大多假设规则采样输入，缺乏对LLM在不规则条件下性能的理解。

Method: 构建了包含1,700个问题的IRTS-ToolBench基准测试，覆盖10种任务类型和13个领域，提供标准化输入和可复现评估协议，适用于任何研究LLM不规则时间序列分析的研究人员独立使用。

Result: 构建了一个填补现有TSQA基准空白的不规则时间序列问答基准测试集。

Conclusion: IRTS-ToolBench填补了不规则时间序列条件下LLM性能评估的空白，为相关研究提供了标准化、可复现的评估工具。

Abstract: Time series data in real-world deployments is overwhelmingly irregular. Observations are asynchronous, missing values are informative rather than random, and sampling frequencies vary across sensors and operational windows. However, existing Time Series Question Answering (TSQA) benchmarks mostly assume regularly sampled inputs, leaving a fundamental gap in understanding how large language models (LLMs) and AI agents perform under irregular conditions. To bridge this gap, we introduce IRTS-ToolBench, a benchmark of 1,700 questions spanning 10 task types across 13 domains. IRTS-ToolBench is designed to be used independently by any researcher working on LLM-based irregular time series analysis, providing standardized inputs and a reproducible evaluation protocol. Code can be found in https://github.com/SanhornC/IRTS-ToolBench.

</details>


### [149] [CONCORD: Asynchronous Sparse Aggregation for Device-Cloud RAG under Document Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15179)
*Xuedong Hu,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Tian Wang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CONCORD提出了一种异步稀疏聚合框架，用于文档隔离的双端RAG场景。通过等待债务控制和证书引导的最小补充机制，在保持答案质量的同时，将吞吐量提升1.66-2.15倍，每token通信量降低两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双端RAG方法依赖频繁远程同步和密集证据传输，在设备-云协同推理场景下受限于实际延迟和带宽条件，吞吐量低下。

Method: 提出CONCORD框架，包括：(1) 等待债务控制机制，基于等待的观察收益决定是否继续等待远程参与；(2) 证书引导的最小补充机制，仅请求确定当前贪婪决策所需的远程证据。

Result: 在Natural Questions和WikiText-2上，CONCORD分别将端到端吞吐量提升1.66倍和2.15倍，每token通信量降低两个数量级以上，同时保持可比的答案质量和困惑度。

Conclusion: CONCORD通过将云视为异步到达的证据源而非持续同步的协同生成器，有效解决了文档隔离下双端RAG的通信瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a pivotal technique for improving language models by incorporating external knowledge at inference time. As device-cloud collaborative inference makes it feasible to deploy small language models on edge devices, a new setting arises in which private documents remain on the device and public knowledge resides in the cloud. Privacy and policy constraints often forbid raw document exchange, creating a document-isolated dual-end RAG setting. However, existing methods rely on frequent remote synchronization and dense evidence transfer, limiting throughput under realistic latency and bandwidth conditions. To address this issue, we propose CONCORD, an asynchronous sparse aggregation framework for dual-end RAG under document isolation. CONCORD treats the cloud as an asynchronously arriving evidence source rather than a continuously synchronized co-generator. Specifically, we introduce waiting debt control to decide whether each decoding step should continue waiting for remote participation based on the observed return of waiting. We also design a certificate-guided minimal supplementation mechanism that requests only the remote evidence needed to determine the current greedy decision. Steps that consult the cloud preserve the same greedy token as dense dual-end aggregation, while the remaining steps commit locally without remote evidence. Experiments on Natural Questions and WikiText-2 show that CONCORD improves end-to-end throughput over baselines by $1.66\times$ and $2.15\times$, respectively, while reducing per-token communication by over two orders of magnitude and maintaining comparable answer quality and perplexity.

</details>


### [150] [CogGuard: Cognitive and Operational Profiling for Proactive Warning in Edge Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15199)
*Zhi Yao,Weihao Chen,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogGuard提出了一种面向边缘智能服务的主动预警框架，通过解耦离线LLM配置构建与在线SLM评分预测，结合前缀对齐KV缓存复用和长度感知分布式微调策略，在教育和操作两个场景下实现高效、低延迟的预警，配置构建时间减少48%，分布式微调时间减少19%，预测MAE分别达到13.4和5.9（百分制）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能服务需要基于长期静态属性和短期动态状态进行主动预警，但现有方案存在两个挑战：1）配置方法领域特定，缺乏跨场景的可复用抽象；2）在异构边缘集群上微调对齐模型因输入序列长度差异导致高同步开销。

Method: CogGuard将离线LLM配置构建与在线SLM评分预测解耦，通过静态-动态配置到评分的共享流水线实现；设计场景特定的配置方法并采用前缀对齐的KV缓存复用来减少重复编码开销；提出长度感知的分布式微调策略，结合对比正则化来缓解异构集群上的工作负载不平衡。

Result: 在教育和操作数据集上，配置构建时间减少48%，分布式微调时间减少19%；在百分制预警任务上MAE分别为13.4和5.9；在最大规模教育场景中，预测误差相比最强基线降低15.4%。

Conclusion: CogGuard通过LLM与SLM的解耦设计及针对边缘部署优化的策略，有效解决了跨场景可复用抽象和异构集群微调开销两大挑战，显著提升了边缘智能预警的效率和准确性。

Abstract: Proactive warning is an important capability for edge intelligent services, where the system predicts whether a subject will successfully complete an incoming task under strict latency and privacy constraints. Such prediction depends on both long-term static attributes and short-term dynamic states derived from historical interaction logs. Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong long-context reasoning for constructing structured profiles from these logs, but existing solutions face two challenges for edge deployment: (1) profiling methods are typically domain-specific and lack a reusable abstraction across service scenarios, and (2) fine-tuning alignment models on heterogeneous edge clusters incurs high synchronization overhead due to the variance in input sequence lengths. To address these challenges, we propose CogGuard, a proactive-warning framework for edge intelligent services. CogGuard decouples offline LLM-based profile construction from online Small Language Model (SLM)-based score prediction through a shared static-dynamic profile-to-score pipeline, and instantiates it in two representative scenarios: educational performance warning and operational task outcome warning. For efficient profile construction, we design scenario-specific profiling methods with prefix-aligned KV-cache reuse to reduce repeated encoding overhead. For edge-side model alignment, we propose a length-aware distributed fine-tuning strategy with contrastive regularization to mitigate workload imbalance on heterogeneous clusters. Experiments on education and operation datasets show that CogGuard reduces profile construction time by up to 48% and distributed fine-tuning time by 19%, while achieving MAEs of 13.4 and 5.9, respectively, on 100-point-scale warning tasks. In the largest educational setting, CogGuard reduces prediction error by 15.4% compared with the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [151] [Attribute Inference from Interactive Targeted Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15209)
*Peihao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了交互式定向广告系统中，用户可见操作与广告活动相绑定所形成的属性推断通道，将其建模为带噪预言机，并通过仿真实验评估了不同攻击方法在多种披露策略下的推断能力，发现披露策略是最有效的隐私控制手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示交互式定向广告中用户可见操作被链接到特定广告活动后，为广告主提供的属性推断通道，量化该通道的隐私风险，并提出相应的防御评估方法。

Method: 将定向广告中的定位谓词、曝光、交互和披露分离建模，构建噪声预言机；使用合成种群和公共数据校准，生成广告语义层；通过仿真器产生地面真相、事件轨迹、披露观测和指标；比较贝叶斯、监督学习、正无标签学习和自适应攻击方法。

Result: 在160次广告活动设置下，贝叶斯和监督攻击的AUC约为0.64（主设置）和0.65（高交互设置）；披露策略是最强的隐私控制手段，聚合报告消除用户级预言机输入，类型过滤和随机化披露减少释放信号。

Conclusion: 本文提供了交互式定向广告中隐私风险的模型、仿真工具和防御评估方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Targeted advertising systems can pair audiences selected by advertisers with ad units that expose visible user actions. When an interaction remains linked to the campaign that elicited it, the advertiser may receive an observation tied to a user rather than only an aggregate report. We model that channel as a noisy oracle for attribute inference. The model separates targeting predicates, exposure, interaction, and disclosure. These boundaries capture the gap between eligibility and delivery, and the gap between interaction and advertiser visibility.
  We build a reproducible benchmark using synthetic populations calibrated with public data, each with known sensitive labels. A generated campaign semantics layer provides topic variants and response priors. The simulator generates the ground truth, event traces, disclosed observations, and metrics. The evaluation compares Bayesian, supervised, positive and unlabeled, and adaptive attacks under common campaign and disclosure definitions.
  The final evaluation uses four topic variants, seven simulator seeds, and two interaction settings. Repeated campaigns with identity exposure produce measurable but bounded inference signal. At $160$ campaigns, Bayesian and supervised attacks reach about $0.64$ AUC in the main setting and about $0.65$ AUC in the higher interaction setting. Disclosure policy is the strongest control. Aggregate reporting removes the evaluated oracle input tied to users. Type filtering and randomized disclosure reduce the released signal. The result is a model, artifact, and defense evaluation method for privacy in interactive targeted advertising. The code is available at https://github.com/P-HOW/Interactive-Ad-Oracle.

</details>


### [152] [Visual-Seeker: Towards Visual-Native Multimodal Agentic Search via Active Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15231)
*Zhengbo Zhang,Changtao Miao,Jinbo Su,Zhaowen Zhou,Chunxia Zhang,Xukai Wang,Ruiqi Liu,Kaiyuan Zheng,Jiansheng Cai,Bo Zhang,Zhe Li,Shiming Xiang,Ying Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Visual-Seeker，一种视觉原生的多模态深度搜索智能体，通过主动视觉推理在复杂开放场景中实现多跳跨模态搜索，在五个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在复杂开放场景中事实依据不足，多模态深度搜索智能体依赖显式语义的简单图像和纯文本证据轨迹，缺乏视觉原生搜索范式，无法进行多跳跨模态推理与搜索。

Method: 提出Visual-Seeker，将视觉视为动态输入而非静态输入，主动关注细粒度视觉细节并在搜索过程中动态收集视觉证据；设计主动视觉推理数据流水线，合成5K高质量多模态轨迹用于模型训练。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的多模态搜索基准上达到SOTA性能，甚至超越多个闭源模型。

Conclusion: Visual-Seeker验证了视觉原生推理与搜索在真实网络环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in many visual tasks, but they often struggle with factual grounding when confronted with complex, open-world scenarios. While recent multimodal deep search agents attempt to address this issue by utilizing external tools, the visual-native search paradigm remains underexplored. Existing methods primarily rely on simple images with explicit semantics and text-only evidence trajectories, limiting the agent's ability to perform multi-hop, cross-modal reasoning and search. To address these limitations, we propose Visual-Seeker, a visual-native multimodal deep search agent via active visual reasoning. Rather than treating vision as a static input, our agent actively attends to fine-grained visual details, dynamically harvests visual evidence throughout the search process. To unlock its visual-native potential, we design an active visual reasoning data pipeline and synthesize 5K high-quality multimodal trajectories for model training. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance across five challenging multimodal search benchmarks, even surpassing several proprietary models, validating robust visual-native reasoning and search in real-world web environments. The code and data can be accessed at: https://github.com/ZhengboZhang/Visual-Seeker.

</details>


### [153] [Mask-Proof: An LLM-based Automated Data Curation Pipeline on Mathematical Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15258)
*Jierui Zhang,Siyuan Tan,Xinhang Li,Longzhuangzhi Lin,Dailin Li,Chengfeng Gu,Xinping Li,Yaxian Hao,Shengjia Liang,Yuxiang Ren,Wenhao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mask-Proof 流水线，通过掩码关键公式步骤并利用 LLM 等价性判断器，构建了包含 292 个问题的 Mask-ProofBench 基准，用于自动、可重复地评估大模型在长证明中的逐步推理能力，评估器与专家标注者的一致性达 96.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多关注最终答案或依赖昂贵的人工评分，端到端的证明生成仍然是开放式的且难以自动验证，缺乏可扩展、可重复的逐步推理测量方法，限制了可信 AI 在证明验证科学进步中的应用。

Method: 提出 Mask-Proof 流水线：将真实数学证明转化为可自动检查的掩码步骤任务，遮蔽关键公式步骤并提供上下文，使用基于 LLM 的等价性判断器（通过重复投票提高稳定性）评估模型重建结果。构建了 Mask-ProofBench 基准（292 个问题，涵盖多个研究领域）。

Result: 在 17 个模型上的实验表明，推理增强型模型比标准模型性能高出 12% 到 27%。评估器与专家标注者的一致性达到 96.8%，实现了忠实、可重复、可比较的逐步数学推理测量。

Conclusion: Mask-Proof 提供了一种自动化、可扩展且可靠的评估框架，可有效衡量 LLM 在长证明中的逐步推理能力，填补了现有评估空白，并支持可信赖的 AI 辅助数学证明验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of mathematical problem solving and can even assist with research-level proofs, yet we still lack a scalable and reproducible way to measure step-level reasoning in long proofs across diverse sources. This evaluation gap limits trustworthy AI assistance in proof-certified scientific progress. Existing evaluations often emphasize final answers or rely on costly expert grading, while end-to-end proof generation remains open-ended and hard to verify automatically. We introduce Mask-Proof, a pipeline that turns real proofs into automatically checkable masked-step tasks. It masks key formula steps, provides the necessary surrounding context, and evaluates model reconstructions with an LLM-based equivalence judge using repeated votes for stability. The resulting Mask-ProofBench contains 292 curated problems across diverse research areas. Experiments with 17 models show that reasoning-enhanced models outperform standard models by 12% to 27%. Our evaluator achieves 96.8% agreement with expert annotators, enabling faithful, reproducible, and comparable measurement of step-level mathematical reasoning. Benchmark, annotations, and code are available at https://github.com/weating/Mask-Proof.

</details>


### [154] [Feature Attribution in Directed Acyclic Graphs Using Edge Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15273)
*Qiheng Sun,Junxu Liu,Xiaokai Mao,Haocheng Xia,Jinfei Liu,Kui Ren,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有基于Shapley值的特征归因方法在处理复杂特征交互和因果关系时存在局限，仅关注节点（特征）归因而忽略了外部性和外生影响。本文提出DAG-SHAP方法，将每条特征边作为归因对象，通过边干预同时捕获特征的外部性和外生贡献，并设计了高效近似计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shapley值特征归因方法即使提供了因果结构，在面对复杂特征交互和因果关系时仍存在不足。它们采用节点中心视角，仅将重要性归因于单个特征，无法同时捕获特征的外部性（externality）和外生影响（exogenous influence），导致解释不合理。

Method: 提出DAG-SHAP方法，将每条特征边作为独立的归因对象，基于边干预（edge intervention）计算Shapley值。同时引入高效近似计算方法以降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在真实数据集和合成数据集上的大量实验验证了DAG-SHAP的有效性。

Conclusion: DAG-SHAP通过边干预的归因视角，能够同时捕获特征的外部性和外生贡献，为复杂因果场景下的特征归因提供了更合理的解决方案。

Abstract: Shapley value-based feature attribution methods face challenges in scenarios involving complex feature interactions and causal relationships, even when a causal structure is provided. Existing methods typically adopt a node-centric view, attributing importance solely to individual features. Consequently, they often fail to simultaneously capture the externality and exogenous influence of features, leading to unreasonable interpretations. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel feature attribution method called DAG-SHAP, which is based on edge intervention. DAG-SHAP treats each feature edge as an individual attribution object, ensuring that both externality and exogenous contributions of features are appropriately captured. Additionally, we introduce an approximation method for efficiently computing DAG-SHAP. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic datasets validate the effectiveness of DAG-SHAP. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-DIVER/DAG-SHAP.

</details>


### [155] [A Formal Framework for Declarative Agentic AI in Business Process Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15291)
*Mohammad Azarijafari,Luisa Mich,Michele Missikoff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGO（Agent-Goal-Object）形式化框架，通过集合论和数学逻辑定义业务流程实体及其交互，构建BP知识库（BPKB），支持结构化查询、增量更新和自动工作流生成，确保路径的健全性和完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赋能使能Agentic AI实现业务流程自动化的潜力，需要以形式化精确性定义BP实体及其交互，现有方法缺乏严格的数学基础。

Method: 基于AGO方法论（Agent、Goal、Object三要素），运用集合论和数学逻辑形式化定义实体类型及其交互关系，构建业务流程知识库（BPKB）。

Result: BPKB支持结构化查询、增量更新和BP工作流的自动生成，并保证了导出路径的健全性和完备性。

Conclusion: AGO框架为Agentic BP分析提供了形式化基础，能够有效支持流程的自动化推理和动态适应。

Abstract: Agentic AI opens new opportunities for automating Business Process (BP), enabling autonomous decision-making and dynamic adaptation. However, realising this potential requires BP entities and their interactions to be defined with formal precision. This paper presents a formal framework for Agentic BP analysis through the AGO methodology. AGO captures the modelling perspective in terms of who is acting (Agents), why it is carried out (Goals), and what the relevant entities are (Objects). Grounded in set theory and mathematical logic, we formally define the AGO entity types and their interactions, organising all definitions into a BP Knowledge Base (BPKB). The resulting BPKB supports structured querying, incremental updates, and automatic generation of BP workflows, while ensuring soundness and completeness of the derived paths.

</details>


### [156] [CODA-BENCH: Can Code Agents Handle Data-Intensive Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15300)
*Yuxin Zhang,Ju Fan,Meihao Fan,Shaolei Zhang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CODA-BENCH是首个在数据密集型环境中联合评估代码与数据智能的基准，包含1009个任务（覆盖31个社区），每个任务环境平均980个文件。评测显示最先进智能体成功率仅61.1%，揭示了当前智能体处理数据密集型任务的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立评估代码能力或数据能力，与真实开发场景（需同时处理复杂代码和大规模数据）存在明显差距，因此需要能联合评估两者的基准。

Method: 基于Kaggle生态系统构建数据密集型Linux沙箱（含数百数据集），智能体需主动探索复杂文件层级以定位相关资源并生成数据驱动分析任务的代码。共构建1009个任务，覆盖31个社区，每任务环境平均980个文件。

Result: 顶级性能智能体在CODA-BENCH上的成功率仅为61.1%，表明现有智能体难以有效整合数据发现与代码执行。

Conclusion: 当前智能体处理数据密集型任务的能力存在显著差距，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Advanced agents are increasingly demonstrating the potential to operate as autonomous engineers, creating a growing demand for evaluation benchmarks that capture the complexity of real-world development. Such environments typically involve both complex code and large-scale data (i.e., file system). However, existing benchmarks usually evaluate code-centric or data-centric capabilities in isolation, leaving a clear gap with real development scenarios. In this paper, we bridge this gap by introducing CODA-BENCH, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate code and data intelligence in a data-intensive environment. We construct a data-intensive Linux sandbox based on the Kaggle ecosystem (containing hundreds of datasets), where agents must actively explore complex file hierarchies to identify relevant resources and generate code for data-driven analytical tasks. CODA-BENCH comprises 1,009 tasks spanning 31 communities, with each task environment containing an average of 980 files, simulating realistic data scale and noise. Evaluations of advanced agents reveal that even top-performing systems struggle to effectively integrate data discovery with code execution, achieving a success rate of only 61.1%. These results highlight a substantial gap in current agentic capabilities for data-intensive tasks and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [157] [Forced Deferral: Manipulating Routing Decisions in Multimodal LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15308)
*Zhongye Liu,Yaopei Zeng,Yurui Chang,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出强制延迟攻击(FDA)，通过对抗性图像触发器降低弱模型置信度，使多模态大模型级联系统将查询路由到强模型，从而迫使系统消耗更多计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型级联系统通过先查询弱模型、在弱模型不自信时延迟到强模型来节省计算成本，但弱模型的置信度直接控制计算分配，这暴露了新的攻击面：攻击者可以操纵置信度使其查询持续被延迟到强模型。

Method: FDA通过学习通用边界触发器，利用温度展平目标函数，将弱模型在触发输入上的token分布推向基于其干净响应构建的较不集中的目标分布，从而降低弱模型置信度。

Result: 在多种数据集、模型系列和延迟指标上，FDA一致地增加了强模型路由比例，并优于图像扰动和提示注入基线方法。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型级联系统容易受到操纵计算分配的攻击，攻击者可以在不直接影响答案正确性的情况下，强制系统非预期地使用强模型。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning abilities, serving a large model for every query is computationally expensive. MLLM cascades mitigate this cost by first querying a weak but cheaper model and deferring to a strong model when the weak model's output is unconfident. However, since the weak model's confidence directly controls compute allocation, these systems expose a new attack surface: an adversary can manipulate confidence so that their queries are consistently deferred to the strong model. Motivated by this vulnerability, we introduce the Forced Deferral Attack (FDA), an adversarial image attack that lowers the weak model's confidence and causes cascades to route queries to the strong model. FDA learns a universal border trigger by optimizing a temperature-flattened objective. This objective pushes the weak model's token distribution on triggered inputs toward less concentrated targets constructed from its clean responses. Across datasets, model families, and deferral metrics, FDA consistently increases strong-model routing while outperforming image-perturbation and prompt-injection baselines. These results show that MLLM cascades are vulnerable to attacks that manipulate compute allocation, forcing unintended strong-model usage without directly targeting answer correctness.

</details>


### [158] [ChatPlanner: A Large Language Model Framework for Personalized Public Transit Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15315)
*Tingting Yang,Chenhao Xue,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChatPlanner框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）结合检索增强生成（RAG）和微调技术，从自然语言查询中提取用户偏好并将其集成到公共交通路径规划的目标函数中，实验表明该框架能够生成更贴合用户个性化需求的可行路径方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化公共交通路径规划因难以捕获和融合多样化的用户偏好而面临挑战，现有路径规划器无法有效理解自然语言中蕴含的细微偏好。

Method: 采用微调后的LLM结合RAG，从自然语言查询中提取路径规划参数并解释用户偏好，然后将偏好集成到公共交通路径规划算法的目标函数中；设计了包含8种人物角色和5种上下文的偏好感知数据集，用于微调和RAG的评分标准建立。

Result: ChatPlanner能够可靠地生成可行解；微调保证了输出结构并学习了通用偏好模式，RAG提供了查询特定上下文以解决模糊表达并校准连续分数，二者结合在路径信息提取和用户偏好解释上达到了最高准确率；案例研究表明该方法能识别现有路径规划器忽略的有价值路径方案。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一种将自然语言理解融入交通优化的新范式，证明了LLM结合微调与RAG在个性化公共交通路径规划中的有效性。

Abstract: Personalized public transit routing in public transit systems remains challenging due to the difficulty of capturing and integrating diverse user preferences into routing algorithms. This paper presents ChatPlanner, a novel framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to enable preference aware public transit routing. Our approach employs fine-tuned LLMs with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to extract routing parameters and interpret nuanced user preferences from natural language queries, subsequently integrating these preferences into the objective function of a public transit routing algorithm. This study designs preference aware datasets incorporating eight personas and five contexts to establish scoring standards for both fine-tuning and RAG. This work conducted three experiments to validate the solutions' feasibility, extraction of routing information and preferences, and solution set quality and completeness. Results demonstrate that ChatPlanner generates feasible solutions reliably. Fine-tuning enforces the required output structure and learns general preference patterns, while RAG provides query-specific context to resolve imprecise or conversational expressions and calibrate continuous scores. The combination of both achieves the highest accuracy in routing information extraction and user preference interpretation. Results based on selected case studies show that by capturing user preferences, ChatPlanner identifies valuable solutions across different dimensions that existing route planners overlook, generating more valuable route alternatives. This research establishes a new paradigm for integrating natural language understanding into transportation optimization.

</details>


### [159] [APEX: Adaptive Principle EXtraction A Three-Layer Self-Evolution Framework for Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15363)
*Ya-Chuan Chen,Tien-Jen Lai,Hsiang-Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出APEX框架，通过三层协同进化（提示词装备、行为原则、工作流拓扑）同时优化AI Agent的三个维度，在单次进化运行中将健康评分从0.300提升至0.570（+90%），仅需4次LLM调用（~270秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Self-Harness框架仅优化提示词装备单一维度，未涉及行为原则和工作流拓扑的改进，限制了AI Agent自我提升的上限。

Method: 提出APEX三层协同进化框架：L1通过失败模式修补优化装备；L2通过成功轨迹蒸馏提炼行为原则；L3通过结构适应度选择进化工作流拓扑。在Joe Agent上基于114条真实任务轨迹进行实验。

Result: APEX健康评分0.570（+90%），提炼6条可复用原则，工作流拓扑评分0.900（+20%），仅需4次LLM调用（~270秒）。

Conclusion: 多维协同进化显著优于单轴装备优化，以极低计算成本实现大幅性能提升。

Abstract: Self-improvement in AI agents has emerged as a key research frontier: systems that modify their own prompts, workflows, and decision rules based on accumulated operational experience. The state-of-the-art Self-Harness framework [1] achieves 14--21% improvement on Terminal-Bench-2.0 by mining failure clusters and patching the agent harness. However, Self-Harness optimises only one dimension -- the prompt harness -- leaving behavioural principles and workflow topology unchanged. We propose APEX (Adaptive Principle EXtraction), a three-layer co-evolution framework that simultaneously evolves: (L1) the harness via failure-mode patching, (L2) behavioural principles via success-trace distillation [2], and (L3) the agent workflow topology via structural fitness-based selection [6]. We implement APEX on Joe [13], a production-grade super AI Agent built on NVIDIA Nemotron and designed as an Edge AI Agent Factory for the NVIDIA Agent Challenge 2026, managing a 15-node compute fleet using 114 real task traces collected over 18 days. APEX achieves an APEX Health Score of 0.570 (+90% vs. baseline 0.300) in a single evolutionary run, distilling 6 novel reusable principles and selecting a research-first workflow topology scoring 0.900 (+20%). Our results demonstrate that multi-dimensional co-evolution substantially outperforms single-axis harness optimisation, at a cost of only 4 LLM calls (~270 s) on a local qwen2.5-coder:32b instance.

</details>


### [160] [S1-DeepResearch: Beyond Search, Toward Real-World Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15367)
*Yao Dong,Xinglin Xiao,Liwei Dong,Xinlong Jin,Zhengbo Li,Heng Zhang,Duyun Wang,Nan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向深度研究智能体的统一轨迹构建范式，通过图基任务构建、智能体轨迹生成和多维验证，合成高质量的长链推理、知识综合、报告生成等轨迹。基于此训练的S1-DeepResearch-32B模型在20个基准上达到开源同规模模型最佳性能，部分挑战性基准接近闭源前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练数据集以搜索为中心，主要覆盖封闭式问答和信息定位，缺乏对证据整合、知识综合、规划、文件理解和结构化报告生成等深度研究关键能力的覆盖。

Method: 提出统一轨迹构建范式，包含三个步骤：图基任务构建（Graph-grounded task formulation）、智能体轨迹生成（Agentic trajectory rollout）、多维轨迹验证（Multi-dimensional trajectory verification），实现高质量智能体轨迹的可扩展合成。

Result: S1-DeepResearch-32B在20个基准（涵盖复杂推理、指令遵循、报告生成、文件理解、技能使用五个维度）上达到开源同规模模型最佳性能，在多个挑战性深度研究基准上接近领先闭源前沿模型。

Conclusion: 联合建模信息获取、知识综合和面向规划的智能体行为对于构建有效的深度研究智能体至关重要。
