Motivation: 间歇聚合反应控制面临三大挑战：无稳态操作点导致无法线性化、内在非线性动力学、以及多相操作引发的状态触发切换。现有方法依赖经验性的切换启发式规则，缺乏系统性解决方案。

Method: 系统性地组合四种实时NMPC技术：(1) 平滑模式混合（smooth mode blending），(2) 高级步进热启动（advanced-step warm starts），(3) 变量缩放（variable scaling），(4) 迭代预算限制（capped iteration budget）。同时提供平滑增益选择和切换曲面定位的实践指导，并显式分析了平滑引入的近似误差。

Result: 在工业气液聚合基准平台上，结合估计器闭环仿真，与PID和传统NMPC基线对比，该方法在有限计算资源下实现了更优的约束满足和更短的批次持续时间。消融实验量化了各独立组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过适当调参，平滑后的切换逻辑与原逻辑在求解器容差水平上数值不可区分，表明该方法在不引入额外启发式规则的前提下，有效解决了间歇聚合过程的实时NMPC可行性问题。

Abstract: Controlling batch polymerization is challenging because the absence of a steady operating point prevents standard linearization; the dynamics are intrinsically nonlinear; and multi-phase operation induces state-triggered switching. This study systematically combines four established real-time NMPC ingredients, smooth mode blending, advanced-step warm starts, variable scaling, and a capped iteration budget, to attain real-time feasibility without ad hoc switching heuristics. We provide practice-oriented guidance for selecting smoothing gains and locating switching surfaces, and we make explicit the approximations introduced by smoothing such that, with appropriate tuning, the smoothed and original switching logic are numerically indistinguishable at solver-tolerance levels. All results are obtained in closed-loop simulation using an industrial gas-liquid polymerization benchmark with estimator-in-the-loop, compared against PID and conventional NMPC baselines. Results show improved constraint satisfaction and shorter batch duration under bounded computation, while an ablation study quantifies the specific contributions of each component individually

</details>


### [266] [A Transformer-Based Mixture-of-Experts Framework for False Data Injection Attack Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15005)
*Ruslan Abdulin,Mohammad Rasoul Narimani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拓扑感知的编码器-only Transformer混合专家框架，用于智能电网中虚假数据注入攻击的联合检测与定位，通过结合拉普拉斯位置编码、扩散偏置自注意力和密集路由的混合专家模块（包含切比雪夫和自回归移动平均图卷积专家），在IEEE 118节点和300节点系统上实现了93.91%的检测F1分数和84.97%的定位F1分数，优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假数据注入攻击通过操纵量测数据误导电力系统状态估计，威胁智能电网安全运行。现有基于数据驱动的方法多依赖单一图滤波机制，难以适应多样化的网络攻击模式。

Method: 提出拓扑感知的编码器-only Transformer混合专家框架：集成拉普拉斯位置编码和扩散偏置自注意力以捕获全局空间依赖；采用密集路由的混合专家模块，由切比雪夫和自回归移动平均图卷积专家组成，根据当前电网状态自适应应用互补图滤波器。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点和300节点系统上，使用NYISO负荷数据进行评估，检测F1分数达93.91%，定位F1分数达84.97%，优于现有基准方法且保持低误报率。

Conclusion: 所提出的拓扑感知编码器-only Transformer混合专家框架能够有效实现虚假数据注入攻击的联合检测与定位，通过自适应图滤波机制适应多样化攻击模式，性能优于现有方法。

Abstract: False data injection attacks (FDIAs) threaten smart-grid operation by manipulating measurement data and misleading power system state estimation. Although recent data-driven methods have shown promising performance, many existing approaches rely on a single graph-filtering mechanism and therefore struggle to adapt to diverse cyberattack patterns. This paper proposes a topology-aware encoder-only transformer mixture-of-experts framework for joint FDIA detection and localization. The proposed model integrates Laplacian positional encoding and diffusion-biased self-attention to capture global spatial dependencies, while a densely routed mixture-of-experts module composed of Chebyshev and autoregressive moving-average graph convolutional experts adaptively applies complementary graph filters based on the current grid state. The framework is evaluated on the IEEE 118- and 300-bus systems using the New York Independent System Operator load profile. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 93.91% detection F1 score and 84.97% localization F1 score, outperforming existing benchmark methods while maintaining low false-alarm rates.

</details>


### [267] [Revisiting The PBH Test: Fast Uncontrollability Certificates via Krylov Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15181)
*Ahmad F. Taha,Mohamad H. Kazma,Abdallah A. Albustami*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过有限时域可达性重新审视经典PBH检验，将状态转移表述为最小能量原问题，证明不可达状态空间机动存在对偶不可行性证书，这些证书无需构建可控性矩阵即可计算，并证明任何此类证书是不可控广义特征向量的线性组合，同时提出了基于Krylov子空间的方法来提取不可控PBH模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典PBH检验需要全局特征分解，计算成本高，难以扩展到大规模动态系统。本文旨在提供一种无需构建可控性矩阵即可高效认证不可控性的方法，并赋予其谱解释。

Method: 将状态转移问题转化为最小能量原优化问题，利用对偶理论得到不可行性证书；证明证书与不可控广义特征向量的线性关系；基于Krylov子空间方法设计算法提取不可控PBH模式。

Result: 证书无需构建可控性矩阵即可计算；证书具有谱解释（不可控广义特征向量的线性组合）；Krylov子空间算法在大规模动态网络（数千节点）上表现出良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种高效且具有谱解释的不可控性认证方法，避免全局特征分解，适用于大规模系统。

Abstract: This letter revisits the classical PBH test through the lens of finite-horizon reachability. By casting state transfer as a minimum energy, primal optimization problem, we show that unreachable state-space maneuvers admit dual infeasibility certificates. These certificates are computable without forming the controllability matrix meaning that uncontrollability can be efficiently certified. We prove that any such certificate is a linear combination of uncontrollable generalized eigenvectors, thereby providing a spectral interpretation without a global eigendecomposition. We also devise algorithms based on Krylov sub-space methods that extract some of the uncontrollable PBH modes from a certificate and demonstrate favorable scaling on large dynamic networks with thousands of nodes.

</details>


### [268] [A Feasible-Velocity Framework for Local Controllability of Nonlinear Systems with Zero-Excluding Input Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15195)
*Amal Bouazza,Mohamed Boutayed,Mustapha Oudani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非线性控制仿射系统在严格排除零输入的状态依赖箱型约束下的局部可控性，引入“容许平衡集”概念，并证明了当无容许平衡输入时，存在一个局部障碍函数沿所有容许轨迹以均匀正速率增长，从而定量阻碍小时间局部可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小时间局部可控性理论及正控制/锥约束控制框架无法处理严格排除零输入的状态依赖箱型约束（如绳驱动机器人中仅能施加正拉力的情况），需要新的理论工具来刻画此类系统的可控性。

Method: 1. 引入“容许平衡集”对参考状态进行分类，不要求控制分布满秩；2. 当存在内点容许平衡输入时，通过局部均匀输入移位将系统恢复为对称控制系统；3. 当无容许平衡输入时，证明可行速度集与原点严格分离，构造障碍函数证明小时间不可控性；4. 提出“容许偏离”概念描述有限时间可达性。

Result: 建立了存在与不存在容许平衡输入两种情形下的可控性理论判据；在无容许平衡输入情形下，严格证明了障碍函数沿所有容许轨迹以均匀正速率增长，提供定量可控性障碍；在欠驱动平面绳驱动机器人上进行了数值验证。

Conclusion: 该框架有效填补了非对称正约束控制系统的理论空白，为绳驱动机器人等严格正驱动系统的可控性分析提供了新的数学工具，障碍函数的存在并不排除有限时间可达性，因此需要进一步研究容许偏离对可控性的影响。

Abstract: This paper studies local controllability of nonlinear control-affine systems subject to state-dependent box constraints that strictly exclude the zero input. Such constraints arise naturally in cable-driven robots and other systems with strictly positive actuation, but fall outside classical small-time local controllability theory and existing frameworks for positive or cone-constrained controls. We introduce the admissible balancing set, an input-space object that classifies reference states without requiring the control distribution to have full rank. When an admissible balancing input lies in the interior of the input set, a locally uniform input shift recovers a symmetric-control system, allowing classical accessibility and small-time local controllability criteria to be applied. When no admissible balancing input exists, the feasible-velocity set is strictly separated from the origin. We show that the resulting separating covector defines a local barrier functional that increases at a uniform positive rate along every admissible trajectory, thereby providing a quantitative obstruction to small-time local controllability. This obstruction does not exclude finite-time reachability through trajectories leaving the barrier neighborhood, which motivates the notion of admissible excursions. The framework is illustrated on an underactuated planar cable-driven parallel robot, for which the barrier is certified numerically over a prescribed state neighborhood.

</details>


### [269] [Dual-Network PINNs for Optimal Control: A Reproducible Benchmark on the Mass-Spring-Damper System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15271)
*Abdeladhim Tahimi,Rinaldo Vieira da Silva Junior*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对质量-弹簧-阻尼系统的最优控制问题，提出了一种透明且可复现的双网络物理信息神经网络（PINN）基准研究。该方法通过两个独立的前馈神经网络——状态网络（通过复合立方-掩码假设精确施加边界条件）和控制网络（无约束）——将线性二次最优控制问题转化为约束优化问题求解。PINN能够复现经典最优成本至四位有效数字，精确满足终端状态约束，点态误差落在两类经典参考方法之间。训练速度比经典打靶法慢约两个数量级。本文贡献在于方法论的清晰性而非新颖性，旨在降低从业者探索PINN最优控制的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN最优控制研究缺乏透明、可复现的基准比较，且对经典控制方法（如庞特里亚金最小值原理、直接转录法）的对比不足，导致从业者难以评估PINN的实际性能与局限性。

Method: 采用双网络架构：状态网络通过复合立方-掩码假设（composite cubic-and-mask ansatz）精确满足边界条件，控制网络无约束。损失函数结合配置点处的物理残差与代价泛函的梯形近似，通过单一标量超参数加权。与庞特里亚金最小值原理（单打靶法）和直接转录（梯形配置法）两类经典方法进行系统对比。

Result: PINN复现经典最优成本至四位有效数字；精确满足终端状态约束；点态状态与控制误差落在两类经典参考方法的误差区间内；训练速度比经典打靶法慢约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于方法论的清晰性与可复现性（附Google Colab实现），而非方法论创新。该工作旨在降低PINN最优控制实践者的入门门槛，使其无需具备伴随方法或两点边值问题的先验知识。

Abstract: This work presents a transparent and reproducible benchmark study of a direct dual-network Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) formulation for the optimal control of a mass-spring-damper system. The classical linear-quadratic optimal control problem is solved by two independent classical methods -- Pontryagin's Minimum Principle with single shooting, and direct transcription through trapezoidal collocation -- and recast as a constrained optimization problem solved by two feedforward neural networks: a state network whose boundary conditions are enforced exactly through a composite cubic-and-mask ansatz, and an unconstrained control network. The composite loss combines the physics residual at the collocation points with a trapezoidal approximation of the cost functional, weighted by a single scalar hyperparameter. On the benchmark considered, the PINN reproduces the classical optimal cost to four significant digits, satisfies the terminal state constraints exactly by construction, and produces pointwise state and control errors that fall within the spread of the two classical references. Training is approximately two orders of magnitude slower than classical shooting on this benchmark, which is honestly reported. The contribution is methodological clarity rather than methodological novelty: the formulation and the accompanying Google Colab implementation are intended to lower the barrier to entry for practitioners exploring PINN-based optimal control without prior exposure to adjoint methods or two-point boundary value problems.

</details>


### [270] [Inexact Warped Resolvent iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15426)
*Raul T. Marcavillaca,Fernando Roldan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非精确扭曲预解算子的结构化单调包含求解算法，通过相对误差准则评估，统一了经典投影-近端和混合外梯度近端框架，并建立了弱收敛、强收敛及线性收敛结果，应用于鞍点问题和计算层析重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单调包含求解算法通常假设预解算子可精确计算，但实际应用中精确计算代价高昂。本文旨在通过非精确扭曲预解算子和相对误差准则，在不牺牲收敛性的前提下，提供更灵活高效的计算框架，并统一多种经典分裂算法。

Method: 采用非精确扭曲预解算子（相对误差准则）构造算法，几何上解释为动态生成割集的松弛投影方法。利用Haugazeau型投影步骤和度量次正则性假设分别推导强收敛与线性收敛。算法框架统一了Tseng前向-后向-前向分裂、前向-后向-半前向、Chambolle-Pock和Condat-Vũ等经典方法。

Result: 在温和假设下建立了迭代序列的弱收敛性；通过Haugazeau型方案实现了强收敛；在度量次正则性假设下得到线性收敛率。数值实验在合成鞍点问题和CT重建上展示了计算优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的非精确扭曲预解算子框架有效统一并推广了多个经典单调包含算法，在保持收敛性的同时提升了计算效率，有望应用于大规模结构化凸优化问题。

Abstract: In this paper we aim to solve structured monotone inclusions using inexact warped resolvents evaluated under a relative-error criterion. The resulting algorithms admit a geometric interpretation as relaxed projection methods onto dynamically generated cuts, extending classical projection--proximal and hybrid extragradient proximal frameworks to nonlinear warped resolvent. Under mild assumptions, we establish weak convergence of the iterates. We further derive strong convergence results by incorporating projection steps onto intersections of halfspaces via Haugazeau-type scheme, as well as linear convergence under a metric subregularity assumption. The proposed algorithm provides a unified framework for incorporating inexact resolvent computations into several classical schemes arising in monotone operator theory and primal-dual optimization, such as Tseng's forward-backward-forward splitting, forward-backward-half-forward, Chambolle--Pock, and Condat--Vũ. Finally, we present applications in saddle-point and structured convex minimization problems. Numerical experiments on synthetic saddle-point instances and computed tomography reconstruction demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [271] [Coercivity and Local Convergence of Physical Learning in Linear Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15443)
*Joshua A. McGinnis,Xinbo Li,Yoichiro Mori*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次对三种物理学习方法（EP、CL和新提出的AL）在小型线性电路中的局部收敛性进行了分析，发现EP和AL执行自然损失函数的梯度下降，而CL具有立方修正项。研究确定了保证指数收敛的强制条件（基于网络结构的秩条件），并证明了该条件在几乎所有期望输出选择下都成立（非退化具有普遍性），尽管存在对称性导致退化的特例（风筝电路）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学习方法利用系统本身的物理特性进行全局信息传递，仅需局部更新规则即可训练物理网络执行计算任务，但目前缺乏对这些方法收敛性的严格理论分析，特别是局部收敛性的数学保证。

Method: 针对线性电路，在离散和连续时间下，对三种物理学习方法（Equilibrium Propagation、Coupled Learning和新提出的Adjoint Coupled Learning）在微小扰动极限下进行局部收敛性分析。利用网络拓扑结构（关联矩阵）构建秩条件作为强制条件，并结合Sard定理证明该条件的普遍性。

Result: EP和AL等价于自然损失函数的梯度下降，CL则具有额外的立方修正项。在强制条件（基于网络关联矩阵的秩条件）满足时，训练损失指数衰减，参数收敛到解流形。强制条件几乎处处成立，但存在风筝电路这样的对称性退化特例。

Conclusion: 三种物理学习方法在小型线性电路中具有局部收敛保证，且非退化条件是普遍的，这为物理学习方法的理论基础提供了重要支撑，也揭示了对称性可能导致收敛性退化的潜在风险。

Abstract: Physical learning methods train physical networks to perform computational tasks using only local update rules, exploiting the physics of the system to handle the global transfer of information. We provide the first local convergence analysis of three such methods -- Equilibrium Propagation (EP), Coupled Learning (CL), and a new method we call Adjoint Coupled Learning (AL) -- for linear circuits, in the limit of small-nudging for both discrete and continuous time. EP and AL perform gradient descent on a natural loss function, while CL follows modified dynamics with an additional cubic correction. Assuming the existence of a solution, we identify a coercivity condition, expressed as a rank condition on a matrix built from the network's incidence structure, under which the training loss decays exponentially and the parameters converge to the solution manifold. We show that coercivity can fail by exhibiting a kite circuit in which a symmetry causes the coercivity constant to degenerate on the solution manifold, but prove using Sard's theorem that such degeneracies are non-generic: coercivity holds at every point of the solution manifold for almost every choice of desired output.

</details>


### [272] [A Conservation Law for Equilibrium Propagation and Coupled Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15444)
*Joshua A. McGinnis,Adam G. Kline,Yoichiro Mori*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明耦合学习（CL）和均衡传播（EP）在连续时间、小推动极限下，可训练参数中存在类质量守恒定律，并证明该守恒在广泛物理相关设置中成立。该守恒律约束了训练动力学，使线性电路在重要设置中实现可靠收敛。最后讨论了该守恒律的实际意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示物理学习方法（CL和EP）中内在的守恒律，理解其训练动力学的基本性质，为这些方法的收敛性和可靠性提供理论依据。

Method: 通过理论分析，在连续时间、小推动极限下证明CL和EP中可训练参数存在类质量守恒律，并推广到广泛物理相关设置；进一步分析该守恒律对线性电路训练动力学的约束作用。

Result: 证明了CL和EP在连续时间小推动极限下存在类质量守恒律；该守恒律在广泛物理相关设置中成立；该守恒律使线性电路在重要设置中实现可靠收敛。

Conclusion: 该守恒律为理解物理学习方法的训练动力学提供了新视角，有助于解释其收敛行为，并具有实际应用意义。

Abstract: In this paper we show that the physical learning methods known as coupled learning (CL) and equilibrium propagation (EP) conserve a mass-like quantity in the trainable parameters in the continuous-time, small-nudging limit. We prove that this conservation holds in a broad range of physically relevant settings. We then show that the conservation law constrains the training dynamics in a way that makes convergence reliable in important settings for linear circuits. We conclude by discussing some practical implications of this conservation law.

</details>


### [273] [Modern Primal-Dual Frameworks for Prior-Free Online Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15501)
*Rad Niazadeh,Rajan Udwani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提供了基于线性规划的原对偶方法的双篇教程，涵盖正则化凸规划框架（Part I）和无LP的通用证书系统（Part II），用于对抗性和随机在线资源分配问题的算法设计与竞争比分析，覆盖匹配、广告分配、库存管理等多种模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线资源分配问题中，传统LP松弛在随机环境下可能弱化或难以求解，需要更通用的分析框架来设计算法并证明竞争比。

Method: Part I：基于LP的凸规划框架，每次到达时求解正则化凸规划，通过KKT条件生成对偶证书。Part II：无LP的通用证书系统，适用于随机结果场景。

Result: 覆盖在线顶点加权二分匹配、带自由处置的边加权在线匹配、随机奖励匹配、可重复使用资源、双面品类优化、配置分配（整页优化）、AdWords、昂贵取消等多种模型，提供了通用的证明模板。

Conclusion: 该教程为读者提供了分析现有算法和开发新方案的多功能工具集，适用于在线资源分配领域的新兴应用。

Abstract: Linear-programming (LP)-based primal-dual methods are fundamental for designing and analyzing algorithms in adversarial (prior-free) online resource allocation. This chapter provides a tutorial on two modern primal-dual frameworks, emphasizing recent developments and contemporary models in operations research. Part~I develops an LP-based convex-programming framework where solving a regularized convex program at each arrival captures the tradeoff between greediness and hedging, yielding a dual certificate via Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions. Because standard LP relaxations can be weak or intractable for stochastic outcomes, Part~II introduces a complementary LP-free framework that provides a universal certificate system for evaluating competitive ratios under such uncertainty. Covering a wide array of models -- including online vertex-weighted bipartite matching, edge-weighted online matching with free disposal, online matching with stochastic rewards, reusable resources, two-sided assortment optimization, configuration allocation (whole-page optimization), AdWords, and costly cancellations -- the tutorial equips readers with versatile proof templates to analyze existing algorithms and develop new solutions for emerging applications.

</details>


### [274] [Service-Induced Congestion in Memory-Constrained LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15555)
*Ruicheng Ao,Jing Dong,Gan Luo,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了内存约束下LLM推理服务的离散时间动力学模型，分析了在持续批处理中请求接纳、KV缓存增长与主动驱逐之间的相互作用。研究发现：同质负载下无驱逐的均衡点不稳定，系统收敛到唯一的极限环，吞吐损失可达50%；异质负载可通过解码长度互质化稳定系统，而非互质长度会引发同步模式导致不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中，每个请求的KV缓存随生成token持续增长，高并发下内存压力内生性地随时间加剧。当内存超限时，系统驱逐活跃请求并丢弃缓存状态，造成计算浪费与吞吐下降。现有工作缺乏对这一动态过程的严格数学建模与稳定性分析。

Method: 建立离散时间动力学模型，刻画持续批处理下的请求接纳、内存增长与驱逐机制。在同质负载下分析不动点与极限环的稳定性；在异质负载下提出两类别共同输入场景的稳定性判据，并推广到多类别与异质输入长度。引入输入主导的缩放机制，分析解码长度互质性对稳定性的影响。

Result: 同质负载下，无驱逐均衡点不稳定，系统收敛到唯一的最坏情况极限环（除测度为零的精确捕获集外），吞吐损失高达50%。异质负载下，互质解码长度可稳定无驱逐均衡点；非互质长度产生同步模式导致失稳。提出了存活多项式机制，将其推广到多类别场景。

Conclusion: 识别了服务诱导的拥塞（service-induced congestion）作为一种结构性失稳机制，并推导了维持高吞吐的调度设计原则：通过异质化工作负载（如互质解码长度）实现去同步化，缓解内存约束下的性能退化。

Abstract: In large language model (LLM) serving, each request accumulates persistent graphics processing unit (GPU) memory during service as its key-value cache grows with every generated token. Under high concurrency, aggregate memory usage therefore increases endogenously over time: the service process itself creates future capacity pressure. When memory capacity is exceeded, systems evict active requests, discarding cached state and restarting them later, which wastes computation and reduces throughput. We develop a discrete-time dynamical model of memory-constrained LLM inference that captures admission, memory growth, and eviction under continuous batching. In the saturated-input regime, the system admits both eviction-free fixed points and limit cycles with evictions. For homogeneous workloads, we show that the eviction-free equilibrium is unstable and that, except for a Lebesgue-measure-zero exact-capture set, the system converges to a unique worst-case limit cycle that is asymptotically stable outside this exceptional set, with throughput losses as large as 50%. For heterogeneous workloads, we prove a stability criterion in the two-class common-input setting and explain how the survival-polynomial mechanism generalizes to multiple classes and heterogeneous-input lengths. Under an input-dominated scaling regime, coprime decoding lengths stabilize the eviction-free equilibrium, while non-coprime lengths create synchronized modes that drive instability. These results characterize when workload heterogeneity desynchronizes completions and helps stabilize memory-constrained serving. More broadly, we identify service-induced congestion as a structural instability mechanism and derive scheduling design principles for sustaining high throughput.

</details>


### [275] [Schattor: Schatten-family methods for deep learning optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15702)
*Bohao Ma,Junyu Zhang,Chuan He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Schatten范数的自适应一阶优化方法族Schattor，统一了SGD和Muon优化器，建立了无维度平稳性保证，并扩展到多块自适应优化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习优化面临异构参数结构、噪声梯度和高度非凸景观的挑战，SGD存在局限性而自适应优化器虽表现优异但缺乏统一理论框架。

Method: 提出Schattor方法族，基于Schatten范数统一SGD与Muon优化器；通过新颖的矩阵鞅矩界建立无维度平稳性保证；开发多块扩展以自适应平衡块间优化进度。

Result: 在随机矩阵优化问题中建立了无维度（dimension-free）的平稳性保证；多块扩展场景下同样证明了无维度平稳性保证。

Conclusion: Schattor为自适应优化方法提供了统一的理论框架，证明了其在各类设置下的理论保证，弥合了算法设计与理论分析之间的差距。

Abstract: Modern deep learning optimization features heterogeneous parameter structures, noisy gradients, and highly nonconvex landscapes, posing significant challenges for both algorithm design and theoretical analysis. Motivated by the limitations of SGD and the success of adaptive optimizers, we propose {\it Schattor}, a family of adaptive first-order methods based on Schatten norms. Schattor unifies SGD and the recently proposed matrix-variate adaptive optimizer Muon within a single Schatten-norm-based framework. We establish dimension-free stationarity guarantees for methods in the Schattor family for stochastic matrix optimization problems via a novel matrix martingale moment bound. We also develop multi-block extensions that adaptively balance block-wise optimization progress and prove dimension-free stationarity guarantees in this more general setting.

</details>


### [276] [Multi-Stage Stochastic Optimization and Reinforcement Learning Approaches for Dynamic Inspection of Infrastructure Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15840)
*Juan-Alberto Estrada-Garcia,Siqian Shen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基础设施系统中多车辆路径与调度联合优化的动态检测问题，提出了一种混合整数随机规划模型和两种求解方法：随机对偶动态整数规划（SDDiP）和强化学习框架。实验表明，SDDiP解质量高但扩展性有限，而学习方法扩展性强且性能有竞争力，揭示了最优性与可解性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础设施系统中的组件面临异质性和随机性故障，检测路径与调度决策需要同时考虑内生不确定性——即故障时间实现受先前检测行动和故障传播动力学影响。现有方法难以处理这种多阶段、决策依赖的可靠性动态问题。

Method: 将问题建模为多阶段随机混合整数规划，同时优化路径与调度决策以应对内生不确定性；提出SDDiP算法（融合对偶近似、整数状态缩减和基于采样的前向模拟）及基于强化学习的框架（学习任务聚类结构和路径策略）。

Result: 在不同拓扑结构和故障模式的基础设施网络上进行数值实验，SDDiP能获得高质量解但面临扩展性限制，而学习方法在强可扩展性下表现出有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 在优化方法与学习方法之间存在最优性与可解性的权衡，研究为实践中选择何种方法提供了指导，推动了多阶段随机路径问题中内生不确定性的建模，并支持关键基础设施系统的风险感知检测资源分配与可靠性提升。

Abstract: We study a dynamic inspection problem for infrastructure systems in which multiple vehicles are routed and scheduled to monitor components subject to heterogeneous and stochastic failures. A key challenge is endogenous uncertainty in which failure-time realizations are filtered through prior inspection actions and failure-propagation dynamics. This yields a multi-stage decision problem that integrates routing, scheduling, and decision-dependent reliability dynamics over time. We formulate the problem as a multi-stage stochastic mixed-integer program that jointly optimizes routing and scheduling decisions under endogenous uncertainty. We develop a stochastic dual dynamic integer programming (SDDiP) algorithm that integrates dual approximation, integer state reduction, and sampling-based forward simulation to approximate cost-to-go functions. In parallel, we propose a reinforcement learning framework that learns job clustering structures and routing policies through interaction with simulated system dynamics and failure processes. Numerical experiments on infrastructure networks with diverse topologies and failure patterns show that SDDiP yields high-quality solutions but faces scalability limitations, while the learning-based approach achieves strong scalability with competitive performance, highlighting a trade-off between optimality and tractability. This work advances the modeling of endogenous uncertainty in multi-stage stochastic routing problems and bridges stochastic programming with learning-based approaches. The results provide guidance on when to deploy optimization-based versus learning-based methods, enabling more effective and scalable inspection planning in practice. More broadly, the framework supports risk-aware allocation of inspection resources and improved reliability of critical infrastructure systems.

</details>


### [277] [Extended Kalman Filter-Based State Estimation for a Nine-Compartment Nonlinear Epidemic Model -- Convergence Analysis and In-Silico Benchmark Calibrated on the COVID-19 Third Wave in Italy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16305)
*Lokman Rachid Melhani,Antonino Sferlazza,Dominique Persano Adorno,Filippo D'Ippolito,Antonino Lo Burgio,Alberto Firenze*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对九室非线性COVID-19流行病模型（双毒株共存、超级传播者、疫苗接种免疫衰减、住院和死亡率），从三个常规报告观测变量（住院人数H、死亡人数F、疫苗接种存量V）重构全部九个状态。理论贡献包括：通过李导数可观测性分析得到闭式可观测性行列式；设计扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）并给出闭式雅可比矩阵和约瑟夫协方差更新；证明误差的局部指数均方有界性；利用校准残差设计噪声协方差并通过NEES和创新白度测试评估。参数失配研究表明直接观测通道在±30%模型误差下保持准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限的常规报告观测（H、F、V）中实时估计COVID-19疫情的全部状态（包括未直接观测的易感者、暴露者、感染者等），为后续模型预测控制提供状态反馈基础。

Method: (1) 李导数可观测性分析推导闭式可观测性行列式，揭示对称参数下秩亏缺及对称破缺参数r2恢复满秩的机制；(2) 基于欧拉离散化动力学设计EKF，给出9×9闭式雅可比矩阵和约瑟夫协方差更新；(3) 通过Reif-Gunther-Yaz-Unbehauen假设证明误差局部指数均方有界性；(4) 基于校准残差设计噪声协方差，采用NEES和创新白度测试评估。

Result: 可观测性行列式|det(O9)| = δ_w * γ_a² * κ * ρ₂ * w₁² * (δ_i - δ_p)² * |r₁ - r₂|表明校准对称参数下（δ_i=δ_p, r₁=r₂）秩亏缺，三阶李导数恢复满秩9。EKF的RMSE范围为0.07%-2.72%。参数失配±30%下直接观测通道保持准确，间接观测状态性能优雅下降。

Conclusion: 所提框架为COVID-19疫情实时状态估计提供了理论基础，验证了EKF在非线性流行病模型中的有效性，可作为未来模型预测控制的状态反馈基础。

Abstract: This paper addresses real-time state estimation for a nine-compartment nonlinear COVID-19 epidemic model with two co-circulating strains, a super-spreader subpopulation, vaccination with waning immunity, hospitalization, and mortality. Time-varying transmission and vaccination rates are known inputs from a companion calibration, leaving the reconstruction of all nine states from three routinely reported observables: hospitalizations H, fatalities F, and vaccinated stock V. The contributions are theoretical rather than in the filter recursion. First, a Lie-derivative observability analysis yields, via a six-step derivation, the closed-form determinant |det(O9)| = delta_w * gamma_a^2 * kappa * rho2 * w1^2 * (delta_i - delta_p)^2 * |r1 - r2|, showing the level-2 codistribution is rank-deficient at the calibrated symmetric parameters (delta_i = delta_p, r1 = r2); the third Lie derivative restores full rank 9, with r2 the symmetry-breaking parameter. Second, an EKF is designed on the Euler-discretized dynamics with a closed-form 9x9 Jacobian and Joseph covariance update. Third, local exponential mean-square boundedness of the error is proved as a full theorem via the Reif-Gunther-Yaz-Unbehauen hypotheses, exploiting the bilinear drift and linear output to obtain a global-radius quadratic remainder bound that extends to bilinear-drift, linear-output systems. Fourth, the noise covariances are designed from calibration residuals and assessed by NEES and innovation-whiteness tests. All experiments use synthetic measurements from the calibrated model, so reported RMSE values (0.07%-2.72%) are methodology benchmarks, not predictive accuracy. A parameter-mismatch study shows measured and directly-coupled channels stay accurate under model error up to +/-30% while indirectly observed states degrade gracefully. The framework provides the state-feedback basis for future Model Predictive Control.

</details>


### [278] [Output-Feedback Boundary Control of Reaction--Diffusion PDEs on Arbitrary Lipschitz Domains: A Target-Domain Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16345)
*Rafael Vazquez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种域扩展框架，用于任意有界Lipschitz域（包括非凸和多连通几何）上反应-扩散方程的输出反馈边界镇定。该方法将原问题嵌入到已知镇定设计的靶域（如球或矩形）中，通过虚拟PDE填补域间间隙，仅需在扩展路径上的边界配备驱动和共置测量。无驱动边界允许存在（只要与靶域共享且边界条件类型相同）。物理状态的适定性和指数稳定性通过限制获得。离线设计数据继承自靶域设计（常系数情形下为闭式解）。在线虚拟PDE仿真等价于全阶PDE观测器。基于n-球的显式Neumann驱动反步法扩展了可用靶域设计。输出反馈通过靶域观测器提升实现，该观测器由经虚拟域中继的共置界面测量驱动。在星形、马蹄形和多连通域上的数值实验验证了设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反步法边界镇定设计通常局限于简单规则几何域（如球、矩形），对于不规则（非凸、多连通）域缺乏系统化方法。需要一种框架，能将已知的简单域镇定设计迁移到任意复杂几何域上，同时处理部分边界无驱动的实际约束。

Method: 1) 将原不规则域嵌入到一个已知镇定设计的靶域（球或矩形）中；2) 在靶域与原域的间隙中引入虚拟PDE副本，通过界面条件与原系统耦合；3) 串联系统状态精确演化如同靶域上的已知闭环系统；4) 物理状态通过限制操作获得，保证适定性和稳定性；5) 输出反馈通过提升靶域观测器实现，观测器由经虚拟域中继的共置界面测量驱动。

Result: 建立了域扩展框架的严格数学理论（适定性、指数稳定性）；推导了n-球上显式Neumann驱动反步律；数值实验在星形域、马蹄形域和多连通域上验证了方法有效性，包括分区植物/控制器实现和共享壁腔配置。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将简单域上的反步法镇定设计推广到任意有界Lipschitz域（包括非凸和多连通），仅需扩展路径上的边界配备驱动，为复杂几何域上的PDE边界控制提供了系统化工具。虚拟PDE的计算成本与标准全阶观测器相当，具有实际可行性。

Abstract: We present a domain-extension framework for output-feedback boundary stabilization of reaction-diffusion equations on arbitrary bounded Lipschitz domains, including non-convex and multiply connected geometries. The plant is posed on an irregular domain whose boundary has actuated and uncontrolled portions. Just as backstepping transforms the plant dynamics into a stable target system, the method embeds the plant in a target domain, such as a ball or a rectangle, where a stabilizing design is already known. Every boundary portion through which the extension proceeds must carry actuation and the complementary collocated measurement. Uncontrolled portions are allowed when they are shared with the target boundary and have the same boundary-condition type. The gap between the two domains is filled with a virtual copy of the plant dynamics, coupled to the plant through interface conditions, and the concatenated state evolves exactly as the known closed loop on the target domain. Well-posedness and exponential stability of the physical state follow by restriction. The offline design data are inherited from the target design and are closed-form for constant-coefficient plants on balls and rectangles. Online simulation of the virtual PDE has the same computational character as a full-order PDE observer, a standard component of output-feedback designs. A new explicit Neumann-actuated backstepping law on n-balls enlarges the available target designs. Output feedback is obtained by lifting the target-domain observer, driven by a collocated interface measurement relayed through the virtual domain. Numerical experiments on star-shaped, horseshoe, and multiply connected domains, with a partitioned plant/controller implementation and a shared-wall cavity, test the designs.

</details>


### [279] [Robust Koopman MPC with Sets Updates for Time Delayed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16485)
*Xinglong Zhang,Xinxin Yao,Xin Xu,Keyou You,Dewen Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向非线性时滞系统的鲁棒自适应Koopman MPC方法，通过在线更新不确定性集来降低保守性，并在理论上证明了闭环鲁棒性和渐近收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒Koopman MPC方法依赖预先估计的不确定性集，但该集合的界限是时变且依赖于状态和控制的，导致保守性过高；此外，尚无Koopman MPC方案解决非线性时滞系统的闭环鲁棒性问题。

Method: 首先通过数据驱动方式建立非线性时滞系统的提升时滞Koopman模型；分析可控性和可观性等基本性质；设计基于管道的鲁棒MPC算法；提出在线更新不确定性集的自适应机制以降低保守性。

Result: 证明了在外生扰动下的闭环鲁棒性以及标称场景下的渐近收敛性；数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒自适应Koopman MPC方法能够有效处理非线性时滞系统，在线更新不确定性集显著降低了传统方法的保守性。

Abstract: Koopman operators have shown significant potential in designing linear model predictive control (MPC) schemes for nonlinear systems on a lifted observable space. Recent advances have tackled the robust Koopman MPC design issue in the presence of modeling errors, relying on the prior estimation of the modeling uncertainty set. However, deriving a robust positively invariant set using a precalculated uncertainty set can be conservative because the uncertainty set bound is time-varying and dependent on the state and control. Additionally, no existing Koopman MPC design has addressed the closed-loop robustness challenge for nonlinear time delayed systems. Thereby, this article presents a robust adaptive Koopman MPC approach with online updates of uncertainty sets for a class of nonlinear time delayed systems. The unknown nonlinear time delayed system is first modeled in a data-driven manner to derive a lifted time delayed Koopman model in the feature space. By analyzing fundamental properties such as controllability and observability, a robust tube-based MPC algorithm is designed for the time delayed Koopman model. The robust adaptive Koopman MPC algorithm with online updates of the uncertainty sets is then presented to reduce conservatism. Closed-loop robustness under exogenous disturbances and asymptotic convergence in the nominal scenario are proven. Finally, numerical examples verify the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [280] [Testing the max-flow min-cut property and the replication conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16543)
*Ahmad Abdi,Tamás Schwarcz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究复制猜想（Replication Conjecture），该猜想声称每个具有packing性质的clutter也具备MFMC性质。通过将问题限制在特定结构（cuboid，其中不可行点的Hamming图度数不超过δ），作者证明任何极小反例的维数不超过δ，从而将搜索空间变为有限。利用SAT求解器，验证了度≤9的cuboid以及最多10个元素的clutter上该猜想成立。此外，为验证MFMC性质，给出了只需检查有限权重向量的理论上界（t≤max{⌈n/2⌉, ⌊n-√(4n+1)+1⌋}），显著改进了之前指数级的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制猜想若成立，将从整数规划、组合优化到交换代数等领域产生深远影响。验证该猜想对于推动这些领域的发展具有重要意义。

Method: (1) 证明任何极小反例的维数不超过δ，将搜索空间限制为有限；(2) 提出验证MFMC性质只需检查有限权重向量（w∈{0,1,...,t}ⁿ，t≤max{⌈n/2⌉, ⌊n-√(4n+1)+1⌋}），改进此前指数级上界；(3) 利用SAT求解器对度≤9的cuboid和最多10个元素的clutter进行验证。

Result: 验证了复制猜想对于度≤9的cuboid以及元素数≤10的clutter成立；给出了验证MFMC性质的更紧的权重向量上界，从指数级降至O(n)。

Conclusion: 复制猜想在限制条件下成立，且所给出的更紧上界为后续验证提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: The replication conjecture [Conforti and Cornuéjols, 1993] states that every clutter with the packing property has the MFMC property. If true, this conjecture would have far-reaching consequences from integer programming and combinatorial optimization to commutative algebra. In this paper, we set out to verify the conjecture for the cuboid of a set-system in which the Hamming graph induced on the infeasible points has degree at most $δ$.
  The family of cuboids of degree at most $δ$ contains a rich source of clutters with the packing property, including all clutters over a ground set of size at most $δ$. We prove that any minimal counterexample must have dimension at most $δ$, thus making the target search space finite. We then use a state-of-the-art SAT solver to verify the replication conjecture for cuboids of degree at most $9$, and for clutters over at most $10$ elements.
  Our computational verification relies crucially on another theoretical result, that to verify the MFMC property of a clutter over $n$ elements, it suffices to check finitely many weight vectors, namely $w\in \left\{0,1,\ldots,t\right\}^n$ where $t\leq\max\{\lceil n/2\rceil, \lfloor n-\sqrt{4n+1}+1\rfloor\}$. The upper bound of $t$ improves the previous best upper bound by algebraists, which could be exponential in $n$.

</details>


### [281] [Restarted Reflected Halpern Acceleration for Augmented Primal-Dual Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16552)
*Benqi Liu,Ju Cao,Wotao Yin,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带光滑项和可近邻非光滑项的线性约束复合凸优化问题，提出了统一的增广原始-对偶框架，包括原始-对偶混合梯度型和增广Chambolle-Pock型度量选择。精确格式退化为退化近端点形式，线性化格式退化为预处理前向-后向形式。利用反射Halpern加速直接分析原始-对偶变量，证明了影子迭代收敛到KKT点，给出了KKT残差和目标间隙的非遍历O(1/k)界。证明了有限识别属于影子序列而非锚定Halpern状态，识别后通过仿射面模型获得精确残差恒等式和局部尖锐性准则。最后证明了在访问重启集上的不动点尖锐性条件下重启锚点的线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为线性约束复合凸优化问题提供统一的理论框架，解决现有方法在增广度量和加速策略上的碎片化问题，同时建立更优的收敛性保证。

Method: 提出统一的增广原始-对偶框架，包含精确格式（退化近端点形式）和线性化格式（预处理前向-后向形式）；采用反射Halpern加速；通过仿射面模型分析有限识别后的局部行为。

Result: 证明了影子迭代收敛到KKT点，非遍历O(1/k)收敛界；有限识别发生在影子序列；在不动点尖锐性条件下证明重启锚点的线性收敛；提供了标量最坏情形示例。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效连接了多种增广原始-对偶方法，理论分析完备，在凸二次规划等实验上验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: We study linearly constrained composite convex optimization with a smooth term and a proximable nonsmooth term. We develop a unified augmented primal-dual framework with primal-dual hybrid gradient-type and augmented Chambolle-Pock-type metric choices, including a fully augmented Chambolle-Pock-type family that retains the augmented quadratic term. The exact scheme admits a degenerate proximal-point form; the linearized scheme admits a preconditioned forward-backward form. These representations allow reflected Halpern acceleration to be analyzed directly in primal-dual variables. For the shadow iterates, we prove convergence to Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) points and nonergodic O(1/k) bounds for the KKT residual and objective gap, with a scalar worst-case example. We show that finite identification belongs to the shadow sequence rather than to the anchored Halpern state. After identification, an affine-face model yields an exact reduced residual identity and a local-sharpness criterion. Finally, we prove linear convergence of restart anchors under fixed-point sharpness on the visited restart set, with local or tail convergence when sharpness follows from local error bounds. Experiments on linear and convex quadratic programs illustrate augmentation and linearization.

</details>


### [282] [Distributionally robust decision-making under ambiguity: case study of water environmental management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16565)
*Hidekazu Yoshioka,Motoh Tsujimura,Yumi Yoshioka,Ayumi Hashiguchi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对环境项目管理中的不确定性决策问题，基于混合移动平均（MMA）过程构建了统一的分布鲁棒随机优化框架，通过散度惩罚处理模型模糊性，并采用正则化方法处理非光滑条件风险价值目标函数，最终通过水文案例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境过程（如径流）存在次指数记忆（长程自相关）和模型模糊性（数据不足导致模型识别困难），现有方法难以同时处理这两类不确定性，亟需统一的优化框架支持水环境管理决策（如防洪、水力发电取水）。

Method: 以混合移动平均（MMA）过程刻画环境动力学的长记忆性，利用其仿射性质简化无穷维随机过程的建模与计算；构建凸目标（含非光滑条件风险价值CVaR）的分布鲁棒优化问题，通过收敛性正则化获得光滑严格凸近似；采用散度（divergence）惩罚概率密度扭曲以表示模型模糊性，保持优化问题的凸性和可计算性。

Result: 在两组参数识别的水文案例中应用该优化问题，通过统计仿真评估了优化动力学的性能，验证了所提方法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个跨学科的理论与应用框架，将分布鲁棒优化、长记忆随机过程和环境管理决策有机结合，为数据稀缺条件下的水环境规划提供了可计算的分析工具。

Abstract: Decision-making under uncertainty is ubiquitous in environmental project planning. Environmental processes such as a streamflow discharge often present a subexponential memory, where the autocorrelation persists for a long time. In addition, optimization problems driven by environmental processes encounter the issue of model ambiguity because of a lack of sufficient data for model identification. To facilitate decision-making for the management of aquatic environments (e.g., flood mitigation, water abstraction for hydropower generation), we formulate a unified distributionally robust stochastic optimization problem based on a mixed moving average (MMA) process. The MMA process is a superposition of infinite-dimensional affine stochastic processes that is seemingly complex, but the affine property helps with the formulation and computation of the optimization. Our problem is based on a convex objective with a nonsmooth conditional value-at-risk measure. We present a convergent regularization to obtain its smooth and strictly convex counterpart. The model ambiguity is represented as a distortion of the probability density of the target dynamics, and it is penalized by a divergence with which the optimization problem remains convex and becomes computable. As a case study, we apply the optimization problem to two cases with identified parameter values. The performance of the optimized dynamics is evaluated through a statistical simulation. This paper serves as a multidisciplinary work covering both the theory and application of distributionally robust optimization.

</details>


### [283] [Chebyshev-Exact Acceleration under Hessian Variation, I: Sine-Jacobi Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16671)
*Dmitry Pasechnyuk-Vilensky,Martin Takáč*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域下基于Chebyshev极小极大终端残差的单梯度实现，分析了前缀精确和终端精确两类方法在Hessian扰动下的ℓ2增益，发现Jacobi坐标方法可实现更优的增益系数，并通过实验验证了有限时域效应在随机曲率、可接纳块边界、时变二次预测和重启成本等方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限时域下梯度方法的收敛性能，特别是在Hessian时变扰动下的终端一阶变分增益，并探索不同实现方式对增益的影响。

Method: 采用Chebyshev极小极大终端残差准则，分别考虑前缀精确（prefix-exact）和终端精确（terminal-only exact）两类实现方式；利用时序谱核分析ℓ2增益，通过Jacobi坐标变换构造不同谱权重的实现，并进行数值实验验证。

Result: 前缀精确Chebyshev递推的增益为A_N^{\rm pref}=4N^{3/2}ε_N^⋆/(√3(L-μ))(1+o(1))；终端精确Jacobi方法（正弦权重）的增益为A_N(J_N^{\sin})=2√c_{\sin}N^{3/2}ε_N^⋆/(L-μ)(1+o(1))，其中2√c_{\sin}≈2.137936<4/√3，表明Chebyshev终端多项式并不能唯一确定一阶Hessian漂移增益。

Conclusion: 有限时域效应带来更低的随机曲率开销、更大的可接纳块边界、更准确的时变二次预测以及更低的端点耦合光滑强凸GLM重启成本。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon one-gradient realizations with the Chebyshev minimax terminal residual on $[μ,L]$. Under time-dependent Hessian perturbations, the terminal first variation is governed by a time-ordered spectral kernel $K_s(λ,ν)$; its sharp $\ell_2$ gain is $A_N$. For the prefix-exact Chebyshev recurrence, $$
  A_N^{\rm pref}
  =\frac{ε_N^\star}{L-μ}
  \left(4N^2+16\sum_{m=1}^{N-1}m^2\right)^{1/2}
  =\frac4{\sqrt3}\frac{N^{3/2}}{L-μ}ε_N^\star(1+o(1)), $$ and this is sharp in the causal two-term class with Chebyshev exactness at every prefix. For terminal-only exactness, Jacobi coordinates give $P_N=2^{1-N}T_N$: the spectrum is fixed at the midpoint Chebyshev nodes, while the spectral weights parametrize the realizations. The sine weights give a final-exact Jacobi method with the same terminal residual and $$
  A_N(J_N^{\sin})
  =2\sqrt{c_{\sin}}\frac{N^{3/2}}{L-μ}ε_N^\star(1+o(1)),
  \; 2\sqrt{c_{\sin}}\approx2.137936<4/\sqrt3. $$ Thus the Chebyshev terminal polynomial does not determine the first-order Hessian-drift gain. The experiments show the finite-horizon effect: lower stochastic curvature overhead, larger admissible-block frontiers, accurate time-varying quadratic predictions, and lower restart cost on an endpoint-coupled smooth strongly convex GLM.

</details>


### [284] [Novel insights into Pareto fronts in multiobjective topology optimization and a comparative study of scalarization strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16792)
*Tom De Weer,Ole Sigmund,Gabriele Eichfelder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对拓扑优化中的多目标问题，比较了加权和、ε-约束与Pascoletti-Serafini三种标量方法，揭示了Pareto前沿由局部前沿片段组成（导致非凸不连通），并证明Pascoletti-Serafini方法能产生更均匀的近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管单目标拓扑优化研究广泛，但实际设计需权衡多个冲突目标。然而多目标优化与拓扑优化两个领域长期共存却交叉有限，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 理论比较加权和法、ε-约束法与Pascoletti-Serafini标量化法；提出拓扑优化中Pareto前沿由局部前沿片段构成的新见解；通过大量双目标算例（体积、柔度、最大应力、动态柔度）进行数值对比。

Result: Pascoletti-Serafini标量化产生更均匀的Pareto前沿近似，而加权和法出现聚类现象；Pareto前沿在拓扑优化中可能非凸且不连通，与文献中常见假设不同。

Conclusion: Pascoletti-Serafini标量化在多目标拓扑优化中优于传统加权和与ε-约束法，能更有效处理非凸、不连通的Pareto前沿。

Abstract: Topology optimization produces optimized structures by minimizing an objective function under a set of constraints. While considerable research has focused on improving and extending such single-objective formulations, design is usually a trade-off between conflicting criteria. This brings topology optimization into the realm of multiobjective optimization. Unfortunately, cross-fertilization has been limited despite the long coexistence of these two fields. This work aims to bridge this gap through three contributions. The first is a theoretical comparison of two common scalarization techniques, the weighted-sum and $\varepsilon$-constraint method, to the more advanced Pascoletti-Serafini scalarization. The second contribution is a novel insight: the Pareto frontier in topology optimization contexts consists of pieces belonging to local fronts. This can cause disconnected and nonconvex fronts, in contrast to what is often seen in the literature. Finally, the third contribution is a numerical comparison of the scalarization methods, highlighting more uniform approximations obtained with the Pascoletti-Serafini scalarization and clustering observed for the weighted-sum scalarization. The paper contains a large number of bi-objective examples, including minimization of volume, compliance, maximum stress and dynamic compliance.

</details>


### [285] [Augmented Lagrangian Method for Mathematical Programs with Second-Order Cone Complementarity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16820)
*Yan-Chao Liang,Chen-Yuan Zhu,Sheng-Jie Zhang,Gui-Hua Lin,Xide Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二阶锥互补约束的数学规划问题（SOCMPCCs），提出了一种定制化的增广拉格朗日方法（ALM）算法框架，并证明其在有界惩罚参数或有界增广拉格朗日函数下收敛到可行点，在可行性和SOCMPCC-非退化条件下收敛到K-稳定点。数值实验验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典互补约束数学规划（MPCCs）在引入二阶锥结构后形成SOCMPCCs，该类问题因标准约束规格（如Robinson约束规格）在所有可行点处失效，导致经典非线性规划理论与算法难以直接应用。受ALM在MPCCs中成功求解的启发，本文旨在将ALM扩展至SOCMPCCs，以处理互补约束与二阶锥约束间的复杂交互。

Method: 提出一种针对SOCMPCCs的定制化增广拉格朗日方法（ALM）算法框架，该框架具有无矩阵实现和强局部收敛特性。通过在有界ALM惩罚参数或有界增广拉格朗日函数条件下分析序列收敛性，并在引入SOCMPCC-非退化条件后证明收敛到K-稳定点。

Result: 证明了所提ALM算法在有界惩罚参数或有界增广拉格朗日函数条件下生成的序列收敛到SOCMPCC的可行点；在可行性和SOCMPCC-非退化条件下，进一步证明收敛到K-稳定点（SOCMPCCs的基本最优性条件）。数值实验（含示例和高维问题）验证了算法的有效性和实际适用性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将增广拉格朗日方法从MPCCs扩展至SOCMPCCs，建立了相应的收敛性理论，并通过数值实验展示了该方法在解决SOCMPCCs所固有挑战方面的有效性，为处理此类复杂约束优化问题提供了一种可行途径。

Abstract: This paper investigates mathematical programs with second-order cone complementarity constraints (SOCMPCCs), which extend classical mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCCs) by incorporating second-order cone structures. SOCMPCCs present significant theoretical and computational challenges, primarily due to the failure of standard constraint qualifications (such as Robinson's constraint qualification) at all feasible points. This difficulty hinders the direct application of classical nonlinear programming theories and algorithms. Motivated by the success of the augmented Lagrangian method (ALM) in solving MPCCs, we explore its extension to SOCMPCCs. The ALM, known for its matrix-free implementation and strong local convergence properties, is well suited for handling the intricate interplay between complementarity and second-order cone constraints. In this paper, we propose a tailored ALM algorithm framework for SOCMPCCs and establish its feasibility and convergence properties. We show that, under bounded ALM penalty parameters or bounded augmented Lagrangian functions, the generated sequence converges to feasible points of the SOCMPCC. Furthermore, under feasibility and additional SOCMPCC-nondegeneracy condition, we prove convergence to K-stationary points, which constitute a fundamental optimality condition for SOCMPCCs. Numerical experiments, including both illustrative examples and high-dimensional problems, are conducted to demonstrate the effectiveness and practical applicability of the proposed algorithm in addressing the challenges inherent in SOCMPCCs.

</details>


### [286] [Functional Gradient Descent with Adaptive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16926)
*Daniel Csillag,Rodrigo Schuller,Pedro Dall'Antonia,Leonidas Guibas,Luiz Velho,Tiago Novello*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应表示的函数梯度下降算法，通过将近似误差显式纳入理论分析，首次在一般设定下实现了可实现的FGD方法，并证明其收敛到驻点或全局最优解，在回归、PDE求解和计算机视觉任务上均优于固定近似FGD和神经网络基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数优化方法（如神经网络）导致高度非凸损失，训练和理论分析困难。函数梯度下降虽具有强收敛性和简洁理论，但因其梯度是无限维的，无法完全计算或存储，现有实现依赖固定近似引入近似误差。

Method: 提出一种自适应表示函数梯度的FGD算法，在优化过程中动态调整函数梯度的表示方式，并将这种近似显式地纳入收敛性分析中。

Result: 在平滑损失下收敛到驻点，在平滑+Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件下收敛到全局最优解；在回归、PDE数值求解和现代计算机视觉任务中，效果一致优于固定近似FGD和神经网络基线。

Conclusion: 这是首个在一般设定下具备收敛保证的可实现FGD方法，兼具理论完备性和实践有效性。

Abstract: Functional optimization problems are typically solved by optimizing the parameters of a fixed representation, such as a neural network, resulting in highly nonconvex losses that complicate both training and theoretical analysis. An interesting alternative is functional gradient descent (FGD), that is, gradient descent directly in function space, which benefits from strong convergence results and admits a clean theory. However, FGD is difficult to implement in practice because functional gradients are infinite-dimensional, and thus cannot be fully computed nor stored in memory. Existing implementations therefore rely on fixed approximations, which introduce approximation error. We propose a new, theoretically-grounded FGD algorithm that adapts the representation of the functional gradients over the course of optimization. By explicitly incorporating this approximation into the analysis, we establish convergence to a stationary point (for smooth losses) and to a global minimizer (under smoothness + a Polyak-Lojasiewicz-type condition) regardless of our approximations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first implementable FGD method with such guarantees in a general setting. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on regression, numerical solution of PDEs, and modern computer vision. Across settings, our method consistently outperforms both FGD with fixed approximations and neural network baselines in efficiency and accuracy.

</details>


### [287] [Exploding and vanishing gradients in deep neural networks: the effect of residual connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17013)
*Vivek S Borkar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用乘法遍历理论分析了深度神经网络中梯度爆炸和梯度消失现象，并解释残差连接在此背景下的作用，通过Furstenberg和Kifer的李雅普诺夫指数刻画来精确描述李雅普诺夫谱及残差连接对其的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中梯度爆炸和梯度消失是众所周知的训练困难问题，传统方法多从启发式角度解释，缺乏严格的数学分析。作者希望利用乘法遍历理论这一数学工具，为这一现象提供更严谨的理论基础。

Method: 采用乘法遍历理论中的李雅普诺夫指数分析框架，并利用Furstenberg和Kifer关于李雅普诺夫指数的刻画定理，研究残差连接对深度神经网络梯度传播特性的影响。

Result: 给出了关于李雅普诺夫谱的精确数学陈述，并定量说明了残差连接如何改变该谱，从而缓解梯度爆炸/消失问题。

Conclusion: 乘法遍历理论为理解深度网络中的梯度问题提供了有效的数学工具，残差连接通过影响李雅普诺夫谱来稳定梯度传播。

Abstract: The well known phenomenon of exploding and vanishing gradients in deep neural networks is analyzed using multiplicative ergodic theory. The effect of adding a residual connection is explained in this context. Specifically, a characterization of Liapunov exponents due to Furstenberg and Kifer is exploited in order to make a precise statement about the Liapunov spectrum and the effect of residual connections on it.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [288] [QPILOTS: Efficient Test-Time Q-Steering for Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14801)
*Yifan Ruan,Chenyang Cao,Andreas Burger,Ali Pesaranghader,Kaveh Kamali,Jaehong Kim,Nandita Vijaykumar,Alan Aspuru-Guzik,Igor Gilitschenski,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QPILOTS方法，通过推理时引导去噪过程而非修改原始策略，解决流匹配和扩散策略在时序差分强化学习中的优化难题，在50个任务上达到90%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配和扩散策略作为表达力强的动作生成器，在用时序差分强化学习优化时存在困难：直接通过多步去噪过程反向传播评论家梯度会导致数值不稳定，现有方法要么丢弃梯度信息、要么蒸馏为单步策略、要么重复微调去噪策略，效率低下。

Method: 保持原始策略不变，在推理时引导去噪过程。每个去噪步骤中，先将带噪中间状态投影到最终干净动作的估计值，再在该估计值上计算评论家梯度，避免在不可靠的噪声中间动作上直接评估评论家。提出两个变体：QPILOTS-U使用快速单点近似，QPILOTS-M通过学习辅助网络进行可微后验采样。

Result: 在标准离线到在线RL基准测试中，QPILOTS在50个任务上达到90%平均成功率，取得最佳聚合性能。在冻结的大型预训练视觉-语言动作基础模型上，在六个模拟操作任务中达到或超越此前推理时方法。

Conclusion: QPILOTS通过推理时引导去噪过程，在不修改原始策略的前提下有效利用评论家梯度信息，显著提升了扩散策略在RL中的优化效果，并展示了在预训练VLA模型上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Flow-matching and diffusion policies are expressive action generators, but optimizing them with temporal-difference reinforcement learning (RL) remains difficult. Effective policy extraction requires exploiting the critic's action gradient, yet directly backpropagating this signal through a multi-step denoising process can be numerically unstable. Existing methods work around this either by discarding gradient information, distilling the policy into a simpler one-step actor, or repeatedly fine-tuning the denoising policy as the critic improves. We propose QPILOTS, a method that leaves the original policy unmodified and steers the denoising process at inference time. At each denoising step, instead of evaluating the critic on the noisy intermediate action where critic predictions are unreliable, we first project that intermediate state to an estimate of the final clean action and compute the critic gradient there. We introduce two variants: QPILOTS-U uses a fast single-point approximation, while QPILOTS-M draws differentiable posterior samples via a learned auxiliary network. On a standard offline-to-online RL benchmark, QPILOTS achieves the best aggregate performance, reaching an average success rate of 90% across 50 tasks. We also apply QPILOTS to steer a large, frozen, pretrained Vision-Language Action (VLA) foundation model, outperforming or matching prior inference-time approaches across six manipulation tasks in simulation.

</details>


### [289] [GRAPE: Guided Parameter-Space Evolution for Compact Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14865)
*Zhiyuan Ye,Xiangyu Zhou,Ji Qi,Hao Zhang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAPE（引导式参数空间演化）框架，通过渐进式参数暴露与稳定化训练，在保持计算量基本不变（FLOPs比1.009x）的情况下，将ResNet-18在CIFAR-10上的PGD-20鲁棒准确率从51.70%提升至56.94%，同时参数量减少约21.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究参数变得可优化的顺序是否会影响最终的鲁棒解，即使最终架构或计算预算被控制。

Method: 提出GRAPE框架，结合参数空间稳定化与渐进式隐藏单元扩展：在当前暴露空间中稳定鲁棒优化，逐步释放新的可优化维度，并使用对抗频谱利用分数引导新增容量流向高压模块。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、ℓ∞威胁模型下，固定结构ResNet-18 AT作为对照，GRAPE将PGD-20鲁棒准确率从51.70%提升至56.94%，FLOPs比1.009x，参数量减少21.4%；序列增长变体在相同最终ResNet-18架构下达到56.52%。

Conclusion: 引导式参数空间演化能在匹配计算量的条件下产生紧凑且鲁棒的参数配置，性能提升不仅来自最终架构差异，还来自参数空间暴露路径本身。

Abstract: Adversarial Training (AT) improves neural network robustness, but most methods train a fixed parameter space from the start. This paper asks whether the order in which parameters become optimizable can affect the final robust solution, even when the final architecture or computation budget is controlled.
  We propose GRAPE, Guided Parameter-Space Evolution, a training framework for compact adversarial robustness. GRAPE combines parameter-space stabilization with progressive hidden expansion: it stabilizes robust optimization in the currently exposed space, gradually releases new optimizable dimensions, and uses an adversarial spectral utilization score to guide newly released capacity toward high-pressure modules. In contrast to fixed-structure AT, GRAPE treats robust model learning as a process of progressive parameter-space exposure and evolution.
  Under the standard $\ell_\infty$ threat model on CIFAR-10, with fixed-structure ResNet-18 AT as a controlled reference, GRAPE improves PGD-20 robust accuracy from 51.70% to 56.94% at a nearly matched computation budget with a FLOPs ratio of 1.009x, while reducing parameter count by about 21.4%. A sequential grow variant with the same final ResNet-18 architecture reaches 56.52% PGD-20 robust accuracy, indicating that the gain is not only due to final architecture differences but also to the parameter-space exposure path. These results suggest that guided parameter-space evolution can yield compact and robust parameter configurations under matched computation.

</details>


### [290] [α-Fair Insurance Pricing: A Fairness Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14898)
*Tianhe Zhang,Xiguang Liu,Peng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出α-FISP框架，通过约束优化在精算公平与团结公平之间实现可调节的权衡，同时保证保险定价的偿付能力，并通过理论推导和数值实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险定价中长期存在精算公平（基于个体风险差异定价）与团结公平（通过风险共担实现跨群体补贴）之间的矛盾，尤其是在细粒度数据使风险区分日益精细化的背景下，监管机构面临保护弱势群体的压力，亟需一个能够显式权衡两种公平性且保证偿付能力的定价框架。

Method: 将保险定价问题建模为带约束的优化问题，在精算公平保费的基础上，施加各风险类别内的交叉补贴预算约束，通过参数α调节精算公平与团结公平之间的权重，从而得到一族解，覆盖从纯精算定价到纯团结定价的连续谱系。

Result: 推导了框架的理论保证，数值实验表明α-FISP计算上易于处理，且与美国各州异质性公平性要求的监管体制高度吻合。

Conclusion: α-FISP框架为保险监管者和决策者提供了一个灵活的工具，能够在精算公平与团结公平之间选择理想的操作点，同时确保偿付能力这一基本约束得到满足。

Abstract: Fairness in insurance pricing remains a long-standing and deeply debated puzzle. On one hand, insurers, driven by profitability considerations, set premiums that differentiate across individual risks to achieve actuarial fairness. On the other hand, insurance serves a critical societal function by pooling risks across a population, motivating cross-subsidization among groups to promote solidarity fairness. The tension between these two competing notions of fairness makes insurance pricing inherently complex, particularly in modern settings where granular data allow for increasingly fine risk differentiation and regulators face growing pressure to protect vulnerable groups. To address this challenge, we propose an $α$-\textbf{F}air \textbf{I}ndividual \textbf{S}olvent \textbf{P}remium ($α$-FISP) framework for insurance pricing that explicitly captures the trade-off between actuarial and solidarity fairness while guaranteeing solvency, a fundamental requirement in insurance operations. We formulate the pricing problem as a constrained optimization task, where actuarially fair premiums are adjusted subject to budget constraints on cross-subsidization within each risk class. This formulation naturally yields a family of solutions parameterized by $α$, tracing a continuum between purely actuarial and purely solidarity-based pricing and enabling decision-makers to select an operating point along this fairness spectrum. We derive theoretical guarantees for the proposed framework. Numerical experiments show that $α$-FISP is computationally tractable and aligns well with the U.S. regulatory regimes featuring heterogeneous state-level fairness requirements.

</details>


### [291] [GRASP: Gradient-Aligned Sequential Parameter Transfer for Memory-Efficient Multi-Source Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14900)
*Mary Isabelle Wisell,Nicholas Jacobs,Aayush Manandhar,Salimeh Yasaei Sekeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRASP方法，通过顺序处理、参数级梯度对齐和迭代微调，在O(1)内存下实现多源迁移学习，在多个持续学习基准上达到93.5%平均准确率（对比集成方法的71.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多源迁移学习面临可扩展性瓶颈：参数融合时需要同时加载K个源模型（O(K)内存），或部署所有模型进行推理，导致资源受限场景下不可行。

Method: 三个关键创新：(1) 顺序处理：一次将一个源模型合并到演化中的目标模型；(2) 参数级梯度对齐：仅选择性转移与目标域优化方向一致的参数，避免负迁移；(3) 迭代微调：在整合下一个源之前，先适应已迁移的知识。

Result: 在三个持续学习基准（Yearbook, CLEAR-10, CLEAR-100，时间跨度10-108年）和四种架构（1.3M-25.6M参数）上，GRASP达到93.5%平均准确率，远高于集成方法的71.7%，且内存消耗保持常数O(1)。

Conclusion: GRASP的顺序机制使得已合并模型可丢弃，能够扩展到任意数量的源而不增加内存，特别适合资源受限部署和持续演化的源域场景。

Abstract: Multi-source transfer learning faces a fundamental scalability bottleneck: existing approaches require either loading all K source models into memory simultaneously during parameter fusion, requiring O(K) memory, or deploying all models at inference time, making production deployment infeasible. We propose GRASP (Gradient-Aligned Sequential Parameter Transfer), which achieves superior knowledge integration while maintaining O(1) memory consumption through three key innovations: (1) sequential processing that merges one source at a time into an evolving target model, (2) parameter-wise gradient alignment that selectively transfers only parameters whose optimization directions align with the target domain, avoiding negative transfer, and (3) iterative fine-tuning that adapts transferred knowledge before integrating the next source. Extensive experiments across three continual learning benchmarks (Yearbook, CLEAR-10, CLEAR-100) spanning 10 to 108-year temporal distribution shifts and four architectures (1.3M to 25.6M parameters) demonstrate that GRASP achieves 93.5% mean accuracy over all datasets and architectures compared to ensemble method's 71.7% accuracy while requiring only constant memory versus K models for standard multi-source fusion. Critically, GRASP's sequential previously merged models and scales to arbitrarily many sources without memory growth, making it uniquely suitable for resource-constrained deployment and continually evolving source domains.

</details>


### [292] [Policy Regret for Embedding Model Routing: Contextual Bandits with Low-Rank Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14929)
*Yan Dai,Negin Golrezaei,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Hypentropy Policy Gradient (HPG)的策略梯度算法，用于解决现代推荐系统中对抗性查询、bandit反馈和模型有限可观测性下的嵌入模型路由问题，将其形式化为具有低秩专家的对抗性上下文线性bandit问题，并实现了$\tilde{\mathcal O}(s\sqrt{M T})$的线性化策略遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统需要动态地将多样化查询路由到多个嵌入模型，但这一实际问题在对抗性查询、bandit反馈和模型有限可观测性等现实条件下缺乏形式化理解和有效解决方案。

Method: 将嵌入模型路由形式化为具有低秩专家的对抗性上下文线性bandit问题；识别出对数-二次策略类以捕捉查询依赖的模型路由；提出Hypentropy Policy Gradient (HPG)算法，能自适应未知低秩结构并在线学习。

Result: HPG算法在理论上达到了$\tilde{\mathcal O}(s\sqrt{M T})$的线性化策略遗憾，其中$s$为专家内在秩，$M$为模型数，$T$为轮次，避免了维度灾难；同时提供了计算高效且无参数调优的实现。

Conclusion: 本文为解决推荐系统中嵌入模型路由问题提供了理论基础和实用算法，验证了在对抗性环境和有限信息条件下高效学习的可行性。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems increasingly rely on dynamically routing diverse queries to multiple embedding models. Despite its practical significance, this problem remains poorly understood under realistic conditions like adversarial queries, bandit feedback, and limited observability of models. We formalize embedding model routing as an adversarial contextual linear bandit with low-rank experts, where contexts are queries, actions are items, and experts are the embedding models working on low-rank latent representation spaces. We first establish that standard regret notions suffer from structural misspecification or statistical intractability, and we identify a log-quadratic policy class that is expressive enough to capture query-dependent model routing, yet structured enough to allow efficient online learning. Second, we propose a policy gradient algorithm called Hypentropy Policy Gradient (HPG). It provably adapts to the unknown low-rank structure under incomplete information and attains $\tilde{\mathcal O}(s\sqrt{M T})$ linearized policy regret -- where $s, M$, and $T$ are the intrinsic rank of the experts, the number of models, and the number of rounds -- thus avoiding a curse of dimensionality. Finally, we also provide an computationally efficient and parameter-free implementation of HPG.

</details>


### [293] [Separable Neural Architectures as Physical World Models: from Mathematical Theory to Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14934)
*Reza T Batley,Andrew Kichline,Sourav Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可分离神经架构(SNA)，将神经网络逼近与张量分解结合，通过解耦局部坐标函数与全局稀疏低秩交互，形成紧凑光滑的偏微分方程求解框架。基于变分SNA(VSNA)满足Lax-Milgram经典变分保证，在高维时空-参数PDE中以代数而非指数级缩放缓解维度诅咒。在7D制造模拟和Inconel 718热-性能反演中，VSNA在标准笔记本CPU上102秒完成百万次蒙特卡洛采样，较全网格有限元基线实现150,000倍加速，并支持100ms内实时逆态重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法求解高维偏微分方程时面临维度诅咒，计算成本随维数指数增长；同时现有神经方法缺乏严格的变分理论保证。需要一种兼具理论保障、高维可扩展性和实用效率的PDE求解框架。

Method: 提出SNA架构：将函数表示为局部坐标函数(原子)与全局稀疏低秩交互对象的张量分解组合；在变分框架VSNA下作为Galerkin试验空间，满足Lax-Milgram定理的适定性、拟最优性、收敛性和稳定性；采用完全因子化的张量原生交替最小二乘(ALS)优化，将计算成本降至维度线性级。

Result: 在椭圆、双曲、抛物型PDE系统中验证了理论预测的代数和谱缩放率；7D参数制造模拟中，VSNA在标准笔记本CPU上102秒完成百万次蒙特卡洛查询，较NVIDIA A100 GPU上全网格有限元基线实现150,000倍加速；Inconel 718热-性能反演中实现100ms内实时逆态重建。

Conclusion: SNA作为连续参数流形的紧凑数学基板，能够实现实时反演、优化循环和快速不确定性传播，证明了其作为"一次求解，任意查询"物理世界模型的实用价值。

Abstract: This work introduces the Separable Neural Architecture (SNA), a function representational class combining neural approximation with tensor decomposition. The SNA decouples localized coordinate functions (atoms) from global interactions governed by a sparse, low-rank interaction object. This architecture possesses a compact and smooth inductive bias well-suited for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). When viewed as a Galerkin trial space under the variational SNA (VSNA) framework, the formulation satisfies classical variational guarantees under Lax-Milgram: well-posedness, quasi-optimality, convergence, and stability. In high-dimensional spatiotemporal--parametric PDEs, the VSNA mitigates the curse of dimensionality by scaling algebraically rather than exponentially. Exploiting an entirely factorized, tensor-native alternating least squares (ALS) optimization framework reduces this cost to linear in dimension. The VSNA is validated across elliptic, hyperbolic, and parabolic systems, demonstrating close alignment with predicted algebraic and spectral scaling rates. We showcase the SNA as a "solve once, query anywhere" physical world model via two engineering case studies: a 7D parametric manufacturing simulation and an experimental thermal-to-property inversion pipeline for Inconel 718. The VSNA executes a 1,000,000-query Monte Carlo sweep in 102s on a standard laptop CPU, yielding a 150,000x speedup over a full-grid finite element baseline hosted on an NVIDIA A100 GPU. It further enables real-time generative inverse-mode reconstructions under 100ms. These results demonstrate that the SNA serves as a compact mathematical substrate for continuous parameter manifolds to enable real-time inversion, optimization loops, and rapid uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [294] [Remember, Don't Re-read: Stateful ReAct Agents for Token-Efficient Autonomous Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14945)
*Faramarz Jabbarvaziri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将有状态ReAct代理（基于LangGraph实现）替代无状态自研模式，通过持久化状态和固定窗口将每次迭代的令牌成本从O(n)降至O(1)，超参数调优降低90%令牌消耗，代码优化降低52%令牌消耗，同时保持优化质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无状态自研模式每次迭代都从头重建实验上下文，导致每次迭代O(n)令牌成本和总计O(n²)令牌成本，效率低下。

Method: 将自研模式重构为基于LangGraph的有状态ReAct代理，使用类型化持久状态通过工具调用接口跨迭代传递实验历史，实现固定大小对话窗口的O(1)成本操作。

Result: 超参数调优任务：有状态代理消耗2,492个令牌（减少90%）；代码优化任务：有状态代理消耗627K个令牌（减少52%），优化质量相当。

Conclusion: 有状态设计在结构上实现了令牌成本降低，并提供足够详细的架构描述供实践者复现。

Abstract: The autoresearch pattern enables autonomous experimentation by having a large language model (LLM) iteratively modify code to optimize a target metric. Its stateless design, however, reconstructs experimental context from scratch at every iteration, incurring $O(n)$ token cost per iteration and $O(n^{2})$ total. This work reformulates the pattern as a stateful ReAct agent using LangGraph, where typed persistent state carries experimental history across iterations via a tool-calling interface. Two benchmarks are evaluated: hyperparameter tuning (15 iterations, small per-iteration observations) and code performance optimization (40 iterations, large per-iteration observations containing full source code and benchmark results). On hyperparameter tuning, the stateful agent consumes 90\% fewer tokens (2{,}492 vs.\ 24{,}465). On code optimization, the stateful agent consumes 52\% fewer tokens (627K vs.\ 1{,}275K) while achieving comparable optimization quality on both tasks. The token reduction is structural: the stateless agent re-reads the full history at $O(n)$ cost per iteration, while the stateful agent operates within a fixed-size conversation window at $O(1)$ cost. This paper describes the architecture in sufficient detail for practitioners to implement a stateful autoresearch agent for their own workflows.

</details>


### [295] [A Comparative Study of Graph Neural Network Layer Selection for Interaction Modelling in Driving Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14956)
*George Daoud,Mohamed El-Darieby*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对19种图神经网络层用于轨迹预测进行了系统比较，发现ARMA、Chebyshev和拓扑感知层表现最佳，并总结出求和聚合优于均值聚合、多头注意力机制、不同跳距赋予不同权重等设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统依赖精确的轨迹预测，GNN已成为建模智能体时空交互的主流方法，但GNN架构设计缺乏标准化，对于哪些图层能有效捕获空间交互和时间动态缺乏指导。

Method: 对19种图神经网络层进行详细的比较研究，聚焦于空间和时间处理能力，在特定超参数设置下评估各类图层的性能表现。

Result: ARMA、Chebyshev和拓扑感知层在19种图层中表现最佳；求和聚合优于均值聚合；多头注意力机制能实现更丰富的交互；对不同跳距分配不同权重可显著提升预测精度。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了实用的设计原则，有助于指导构建更具可解释性和有效性的轨迹预测模型。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems rely on precise trajectory prediction to plan safe and efficient movement. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become a promising approach for modelling spatiotemporal interactions among road agents. However, designing GNN architectures for trajectory prediction remains non-standardized, with little guidance on which graph layers effectively capture spatial interactions and temporal dynamics. This paper offers a detailed comparative study of 19 graph layer types, focusing on their spatial and temporal processing capabilities to discover the most effective architectures for trajectory prediction. Within the explored hyperparameter setting, we highlight five standout layer combinations, with ARMA, Chebyshev, and topology-aware layers consistently performing better than others. Beyond performance metrics, our findings yield practical design principles: sum-based aggregation is more effective than mean-based methods, multi-head attention mechanisms enable richer interactions, and assigning different weights to different hop distances significantly improves prediction accuracy. These findings offer useful guidance for designing more interpretable and effective trajectory prediction models.

</details>


### [296] [Leveraging Physiological Signals to Predict Exam Outcomes with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14960)
*Lala Yamazaki,Ramchandra Rimal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习模型分析考试期间的生理数据（皮电活动、心率、皮肤温度），预测考试成绩。研究发现深度学习模型虽能捕捉复杂关系，但随机森林等简单模型在性能和可解释性上更具优势，Transformer展现了与LSTM/GRU相当的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生理信号与学业表现之间的关联，通过生理数据预测考试成绩，以理解影响学生心理健康的压力因素，并促进利用生理数据改善学生福祉和学业成果。

Method: 收集考试期间的皮电活动、心率和皮肤温度等生理压力指标；采用多种机器学习模型，包括逻辑回归、随机森林、支持向量机等标准模型，以及Transformer、LSTM、GRU等高级架构；使用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数评估模型性能。

Result: 深度学习模型擅长捕捉生理数据中的复杂关系，但随机森林等简单模型在某些情况下表现更优且计算效率更高、可解释性更强；Transformer展现了与LSTM和GRU相当的性能，在处理数值数据方面具有适应性。

Conclusion: 应依据问题目标尝试多种模型，在精度、效率和可解释性之间取得平衡；生理信号与学业表现之间存在可预测的关联，这有助于理解学生压力并促进其心理健康。

Abstract: This study investigates the application of machine learning models to predict exam outcomes using physiological data collected during examination sessions. Physiological stress indicators, including electrodermal activity, heart rate, and skin temperature, were analyzed to uncover their association with academic performance. A variety of machine learning approaches were employed, ranging from standard models like logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machines to more advanced architectures, including transformers, long short-term memory (LSTM), and gated recurrent unit (GRU) models. This diversity aimed to capture the complex interactions within the data effectively. A key focus was assessing the adaptability of transformers in processing numerical data and evaluating their performance in this novel context. Standard performance metrics, such as accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score, were used to compare model efficacy. The experimental results demonstrate that while deep learning models generally excel at capturing complex relationships in physiological data, simpler models like random forests can sometimes achieve superior performance while offering computational efficiency and interpretability. Furthermore, transformers demonstrated notable versatility, showcasing performances comparable to those of the LSTM and GRU models. This research underscores the importance of experimenting with a broad class of models that align with the objectives of the problem at hand, balancing precision, efficiency, and interpretability. By elucidating the relationships between physiological signals and academic performance, this study contributes to understanding stressors affecting students' mental health. It further promotes leveraging physiological data to enhance student well-being and academic outcomes.

</details>


### [297] [Benchmarking Instance-Dependent Label Noise with Controlled Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14965)
*Shadman Islam,Agustinus Kristiadi,Mostafa Milani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CILN，一个通过受控输入损坏生成合成实例依赖标签噪声（IDN）的基准生成框架。使用多样化投票者对损坏实例进行标注，生成的基准数据集能够显式且可控地呈现模糊性的来源和严重程度。在CIFAR-10、MNIST和Adult上构建了90个基准设置，实验表明生成的基准具有真实的实例依赖噪声、多样的混淆结构，且在某些情况下比现有合成IDN基准更接近人类不确定性。进一步证明，损坏介导的IDN能够暴露Co-Teaching和DivideMix等流行噪声标签学习方法的失败模式，而这些模式在同等水平的rater-fallibility噪声下未被观察到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成实例依赖标签噪声（IDN）基准通常通过不完美的标注者或分类器生成噪声，模糊性的来源不明确。作者希望建立一个通过受控输入损坏生成IDN的框架，使得噪声的来源和严重程度都可解释、可控，从而更系统地研究噪声结构对算法行为的影响。

Method: 提出CILN框架：(1) 对原始实例施加受控的输入损坏（如高斯噪声、遮挡等不同家族），产生损坏实例；(2) 使用一个多样化的投票者池（多个分类器）对损坏实例进行标注，根据投票分布生成标签噪声；(3) 在CIFAR-10、MNIST和Adult数据集上构建涵盖多个损坏家族和严重程度的90个基准设置。

Result: (1) CILN生成的基准表现出真实的实例依赖噪声和多样的混淆结构；(2) 在CIFAR-10上，CILN能产生比现有合成IDN基准更接近人类不确定性的标签分布；(3) 损坏介导的IDN能暴露Co-Teaching和DivideMix等方法的失败模式，而这些模式在同等噪声率的rater-fallibility噪声下未出现。

Conclusion: 噪声结构（不仅限于噪声率）在基准难度和算法行为中扮演重要角色。CILN通过显式且可控的模糊性生成，为研究不同实例难度来源下的噪声标签学习提供了一个互补的基准测试框架。

Abstract: Synthetic instance-dependent label noise (IDN) benchmarks are widely used to evaluate noisy-label learning methods, yet existing approaches typically generate noise through imperfect annotators or classifier raters, leaving the source of ambiguity implicit. We introduce CILN, a benchmark generation framework that creates IDN through controlled input corruptions. A diverse voter pool labels corrupted instances, producing benchmark datasets in which both the source and severity of ambiguity are explicit and controllable. Using CIFAR10, MNIST, and Adult, we construct 90 benchmark settings spanning multiple corruption families and severity levels. Our experiments show that the resulting benchmarks exhibit genuine instance-dependent noise, provide diverse confusion structures, and, on CIFAR-10, can produce label distributions that are closer to human uncertainty than an existing synthetic IDN benchmark. We further demonstrate that corruption-mediated IDN can expose failure modes of popular noisy-label learning methods, including Co-Teaching and DivideMix, that are not observed under comparable levels of rater-fallibility noise. These findings suggest that noise structure, not only noise rate, plays an important role in benchmark difficulty and algorithm behavior. By making ambiguity generation explicit and controllable, CILN provides a complementary benchmarking framework for studying noisy-label learning under diverse sources of instance difficulty.

</details>


### [298] [Zero-order Parameter-free Optimization for LMO-based Methods: Novel Approach for Efficient Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14970)
*Dmitriy Bystrov,Daniil Medyakov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaNAGED方法，统一了零阶优化、参数自适应调优和非欧几里得更新几何，用于大规模LLM微调，在OPT-1.3B上验证了收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM微调中，反向传播的内存开销巨大；零阶优化虽节省内存但对步长和平滑参数敏感，需要昂贵的任务特定调参；参数无关优化可自适应调整参数，且几何感知更新能更好处理参数块异质性。

Method: 提出AdaNAGED，融合了无梯度训练（ZO优化）、自适应调参（PF优化）和非欧几里得更新几何（基于线性最小化预言机LMO）。

Result: 建立了收敛性理论保证，并在OPT-1.3B大语言模型微调任务上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: AdaNAGED有效解决了零阶优化中参数敏感性问题，为大规模LLM微调提供了一种内存高效、无需繁琐调参的优化方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) has become a central application of modern optimization, enabling pretrained models to adapt to diverse downstream tasks and domain-specific data. A major obstacle in large-scale fine-tuning is the memory overhead of backpropagation, which requires storing activations, gradients, and optimizer states. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a memory-efficient alternative, but its performance is highly sensitive to the stepsize and smoothing parameter, often requiring costly task-specific tuning. Parameter-free (PF) optimization addresses this issue by adapting algorithmic parameters without prior knowledge of problem-dependent constants. Moreover, large-scale fine-tuning can benefit from geometry-aware updates that account for the heterogeneous structure of parameter blocks, which can be modeled through methods that exploit linear minimization oracle (LMO). In this work, we study PF adaptation for LMO-based ZO optimization and introduce $\texttt{AdaNAGED}$, a method that unifies gradient-free training, adaptive tuning, and non-Euclidean update geometry. We establish convergence guarantees and validate the method on large-scale LLM fine-tuning task with $\texttt{OPT}-1.3\mathrm{B}$ model.

</details>


### [299] [FastMix: Fast Data Mixture Optimization via Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14971)
*Haoru Tan,Sitong Wu,Yanfeng Chen,Jun Xia,Ruobing Xie,Bin Xia,Xingwu Sun,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FASTMIX提出了一种自动化数据混合发现框架，仅需训练单个代理模型，通过将混合选择重新表述为双层优化问题，并证明优化混合比等价于在均匀源采样下分配每源损失权重，从而实现对混合系数和模型参数的联合梯度优化，显著提升预训练和后训练阶段的效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型模型依赖大规模多样化数据集，但如何确定预训练和后训练中的最优数据混合比例仍是一个重要的开放问题。现有方法依赖预定义启发式规则或资源密集型模拟，效率和可扩展性不足。

Method: FASTMIX将数据混合选择重新表述为双层优化问题，证明优化混合比等价于在均匀源采样下为每个数据源分配可微的损失权重。通过交替执行内循环（按当前混合比采样数据更新模型参数）和外循环（基于验证反馈更新混合比），实现混合系数与模型参数的联合梯度优化。

Result: 在预训练和后训练实验中都优于基线方法，同时显著降低了搜索成本。

Conclusion: FASTMIX是一种高效、可扩展的自动化数据混合发现框架，通过双层优化和梯度驱动的方式解决了数据混合优化这一开放问题，在性能和搜索成本上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: While large and diverse datasets have driven recent advances in large models, identifying the optimal data mixture for pre-training and post-training remains a significant open problem. We address this challenge with FASTMIX, a novel framework that automates data mixture discovery while training only a single proxy model. Instead of relying on predefined heuristics or resource-intensive simulations, FASTMIX jointly optimizes mixture coefficients and model parameters, substantially improving efficiency and scalability over prior approaches. At the core of FASTMIX is a reformulation of mixture selection as a bilevel optimization problem. Under this reformulation, we show that optimizing mixture ratios is mathematically equivalent to assigning per-source loss weights under uniform source sampling. This embeds the mixture coefficients directly into the differentiable iterative optimization objective, enabling efficient, gradient-based optimization of both mixture and model. To solve the optimization problem, FASTMIX implements an approximate iterative optimization procedure, alternating between (i) updating model parameters on data sampled according to current mixture ratios (inner loop) and (ii) updating mixture ratios based on validation feedback (outer loop). Across pre- and post-training, FASTMIX outperforms baselines while drastically reducing search cost. Code (https://github.com/hrtan/fastmix)

</details>


### [300] [Rational Sparse Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14990)
*Naiyu Yin,Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rational Sparse Autoencoder (RSAE)，用可训练的有理函数替换SAE中固定的编码器非线性激活（如ReLU、JumpReLU、TopK），通过两阶段流程（初始化+微调）在多项指标上严格优于现有SAE基线，且仅增加少量标量参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE家族使用固定的编码器非线性（ReLU、JumpReLU、TopK），硬编码了特定的稀疏机制，扭曲了重构-稀疏度权衡，缺乏灵活适应预激活几何分布的能力。

Method: 两阶段流程：(1) 初始化阶段：复制预训练SAE权重，通过松弛Remez交换算法在合成数据上拟合有理系数并校准尺度参数；(2) 微调阶段：在标准稀疏正则化重构目标下继续训练。

Result: 在三个开源语言模型的残差流激活上，RSAE在所有三种基线激活族（ReLU、JumpReLU、TopK）下均严格提升重构指标和下游行为指标，且不牺牲特征级可解释性；仅增加少量标量参数，可在单张消费级GPU上数分钟内完成。

Conclusion: RSAE通过可训练有理激活函数统一并改进了现有SAE家族，提供了更灵活的自适应稀疏机制，且计算开销极小。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are standard tools for mechanistic interpretability, but current SAE families are constrained by fixed encoder nonlinearities such as ReLU, JumpReLU, and TopK. This hard-codes a particular sparsity mechanism into the model and can distort the reconstruction-versus-sparsity trade-off. We introduce the Rational Sparse Autoencoder (RSAE), which replaces the fixed encoder activation with a trainable rational function. Rational activations are flexible enough to uniformly approximate the activation primitives used by existing SAE families on compact domains (for TopK, the thresholded gate obtained after a separating top-k threshold is supplied), while also providing a richer function class for adapting to the observed pre-activation geometry. We realise this idea through a two-stage pipeline: an initialisation procedure that copies the pre-trained baseline SAE weights, plugs in rational coefficients obtained by the relaxed Remez exchange on synthetic data, and calibrates the scale parameters along with the rational coefficients; followed by a fine-tuning step under the standard sparsity-regularised reconstruction objective. Empirically, on residual-stream activations of three open-weight language models and across all three baseline activation families, the RSAE strictly improves on it after the fine-tuning step, both on reconstruction-side metrics and on downstream-behaviour metrics, without sacrificing feature-level interpretability under sparse probing. These gains are consistent across host language models, across baseline activation families, and across the full range of baseline sparsity we tested, while the upgrade itself adds only a handful of scalar parameters per autoencoder and runs in minutes on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [301] [Unlocking Latent Dimensions: Exploring Representations of Large-Scale X-ray Scattering Data using Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14999)
*Monika Choudhary,Xiaoya Chong,Runbo Jiang,Wiebke Koepp,Petrus H. Zwart,Damon English,Gregory M. Su,Eric Schaible,Chenhui Zhu,Mostafa Nassr,Noah P. Wamble,Kelvin Kam-Yun Li,Jonathan M. Chan,Jose Carlos Diaz,Cameron McKay,Lynn Katz,Benny Freeman,Guillaume Freychet,Yevgen Matviychuk,Eliot Gann,Daniel B. Allan,Benedikt Sochor,Frank Schluenzen,Stephan V. Roth,Ethan Crumlin,Dylan McReynolds,Tanny Chavez,Alexander Hexemer*

Main category: cs.LG
